Project Assistant: complete source for review (e6e45b7 2026-11-19 + uncommitted changes)
Files: extension/ (Chrome extension, Manifest V3; third-party libraries in extension/vendor/ are left out),
server/setup-webdav.sh (Proxmox container setup), server/assistant-ocr-index.py (OCR / mail / NAS-only text job
on the NAS) and server/setup-drive-copy.sh (daily Google Drive copy with rclone).
Tests live in tests/ in the repository and are not included here.

// ============================================================================
//  NAS Uploader — your settings. Edit the values below, save, then reload the
//  extension (chrome://extensions → NAS Uploader → reload icon).
// ============================================================================
//
//  SECURITY: anyone who can use this Chrome profile can use these credentials.
//  Use a NAS account that can only write to the shared folder you upload into,
//  and never commit your real password to a public repository.

export const CONFIG = {
  // The WebDAV address of your NAS as reachable from the internet. Examples:
  //   Synology (WebDAV Server package, HTTPS):  'https://mynas.synology.me:5006/'
  //   QNAP (WebDAV over HTTPS):                 'https://mynas.myqnapcloud.com:8081/'
  //   TrueNAS / Apache / other WebDAV:          'https://nas.example.com/webdav/'
  //   Nextcloud:                                'https://cloud.example.com/remote.php/dav/files/USERNAME/'
  serverUrl: 'https://your-nas.example.com:5006/',

  username: 'your-nas-username',

  // Put the password here to have it built in. Leave it as '' to be asked for it
  // once per browser session instead (it is then kept in memory only).
  password: 'your-nas-password',

  // Folder that opens first, e.g. '/home' or '/Backups'. '/' is the WebDAV root.
  startFolder: '/',

  // Files uploaded at the same time (1–6). 3 is a good default.
  parallelUploads: 3,
};


===== FILE: extension/background.js (288 lines) =====
// Service worker: routes messages between the Outlook tab, the side panel, the meeting window and the
// engine (offscreen document), runs the one-minute timer, keeps the toolbar badge up to date, and
// opens the NAS file manager (app.html) and the meeting window.

import { getSettings, getAll } from './lib/db.js';
import { splitList } from './lib/text.js';

const OUTLOOK_URLS = [
  'https://outlook.office.com/*',
  'https://outlook.office365.com/*',
  'https://outlook.live.com/*',
  'https://outlook.cloud.microsoft/*',
];

const APP_URL = chrome.runtime.getURL('app.html');
const MEETING_URL = chrome.runtime.getURL('meeting.html');
const RECORDER_URL = chrome.runtime.getURL('recorder.html');
const AGENT_URL = chrome.runtime.getURL('agent.html');

// ---------- engine (offscreen document) ----------

let creating = null;
async function ensureEngine() {
  const contexts = await chrome.runtime.getContexts({ contextTypes: ['OFFSCREEN_DOCUMENT'] });
  if (contexts.length) return;
  if (!creating) {
    creating = chrome.offscreen
      .createDocument({
        url: 'engine.html',
        reasons: ['DOM_PARSER', 'WORKERS', 'BLOBS'],
        justification: 'Indexes project files and prepares email drafts in the background.',
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!String(err.message).includes('single offscreen')) throw err;
      })
      .finally(() => {
        creating = null;
      });
  }
  await creating;
}

async function toEngine(payload) {
  await ensureEngine();
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 20; attempt++) {
    try {
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ ...payload, to: 'engine' });
      if (res !== undefined) return res;
    } catch (err) {
      if (!/Receiving end does not exist/i.test(err.message)) throw err;
    }
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150)); // engine still loading its modules
  }
  throw new Error('engine did not respond');
}

// ---------- NAS file manager tab and meeting window ----------

async function findTab(url) {
  try {
    const contexts = await chrome.runtime.getContexts({ contextTypes: ['TAB'] });
    const ctx = contexts.find((c) => c.documentUrl && c.documentUrl.startsWith(url) && c.tabId >= 0);
    if (ctx) return { tabId: ctx.tabId, windowId: ctx.windowId };
  } catch {
    // getContexts unavailable — fall through and open a new tab.
  }
  return null;
}

/** Opens the NAS file manager in a full tab (a popup would close and kill uploads), or focuses it. */
async function openApp(hash = '') {
  const existing = await findTab(APP_URL);
  if (existing) {
    await chrome.tabs.update(existing.tabId, { active: true, ...(hash ? { url: APP_URL + hash } : {}) });
    if (existing.windowId >= 0) await chrome.windows.update(existing.windowId, { focused: true });
    return existing;
  }
  const tab = await chrome.tabs.create({ url: APP_URL + hash });
  return { tabId: tab.id, windowId: tab.windowId };
}

/** The meeting assistant and the recorder get their own small windows, so they keep going when the side panel closes. */
async function openWindow(url, width, height) {
  const existing = await findTab(url);
  if (existing) {
    if (existing.windowId >= 0) await chrome.windows.update(existing.windowId, { focused: true });
    return existing;
  }
  const win = await chrome.windows.create({ url, type: 'popup', width, height });
  return { windowId: win.id, tabId: win.tabs?.[0]?.id };
}
const openMeeting = () => openWindow(MEETING_URL, 460, 780);
const openRecorder = () => openWindow(RECORDER_URL, 420, 640);

/** The agent window, set to work in the tab the user was looking at. */
async function openAgent({ task = '' } = {}) {
  const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, lastFocusedWindow: true, windowType: 'normal' }).catch(() => []);
  const existing = await findTab(AGENT_URL);
  if (existing) {
    if (existing.windowId >= 0) await chrome.windows.update(existing.windowId, { focused: true });
    // A task from the chat: the open agent window takes it (unless it is busy with another one).
    if (task) {
      const r = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ to: 'agent', type: 'agent.task', task, tabId: tab?.id }).catch(() => null);
      return { ...existing, ...(r || {}) };
    }
    return existing;
  }
  const hash = new URLSearchParams();
  if (tab?.id) hash.set('tab', String(tab.id));
  if (task) {
    hash.set('task', task);
    hash.set('go', '1');
  }
  const win = await chrome.windows.create({ url: `${AGENT_URL}${hash.size ? `#${hash}` : ''}`, type: 'popup', width: 500, height: 840 });
  return { windowId: win.id, tabId: win.tabs?.[0]?.id, started: Boolean(task) };
}

/** The page the user has open (the last focused normal window): title, address, visible and selected text. */
async function readOpenPage() {
  // The active tab; when that isn't a web page (an extension page, chrome://), the web page used last in that window.
  const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ lastFocusedWindow: true, windowType: 'normal' }).catch(() => []);
  const web = tabs.filter((t) => /^https?:/i.test(t.url || ''));
  const tab = web.find((t) => t.active) || web.sort((a, b) => (b.lastAccessed || 0) - (a.lastAccessed || 0))[0];
  if (!tab?.id) return { error: 'No web page is open in Chrome (open the page, then ask again).' };
  try {
    const [res] = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
      target: { tabId: tab.id },
      func: (max) => {
        const sel = String(window.getSelection?.() || '').trim();
        const text = (document.body?.innerText || '').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
        return { title: document.title, url: location.href, selected: sel.slice(0, 4000), text: text.length > max ? `${text.slice(0, max)}\n[…shortened]` : text };
      },
      args: [12000],
    });
    return res?.result || { error: 'The page could not be read.' };
  } catch (err) {
    return { error: `Chrome doesn't let extensions read this page (${err.message}).`, title: tab.title, url: tab.url };
  }
}

// ---------- Outlook ----------

async function outlookUrls() {
  const s = await getSettings();
  return [...OUTLOOK_URLS, ...splitList(s.extraOutlookUrls)];
}

async function outlookTabs() {
  const urls = await outlookUrls();
  const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ url: urls }).catch(() => []);
  return tabs.sort((a, b) => (b.active ? 1 : 0) - (a.active ? 1 : 0) || (b.lastAccessed || 0) - (a.lastAccessed || 0));
}

async function toOutlook(payload) {
  const tabs = await outlookTabs();
  if (!tabs.length) return { error: 'No Outlook Web tab is open.' };
  for (const tab of tabs) {
    try {
      const res = await chrome.tabs.sendMessage(tab.id, payload);
      if (res) return { ...res, tabId: tab.id };
    } catch {
      /* content script not loaded in this tab yet */
    }
  }
  return { error: 'Outlook tab found but the extension is not active in it — reload the Outlook tab once.' };
}

// On-premises Outlook (custom URL) needs its content script registered at runtime.
async function syncExtraContentScripts() {
  const s = await getSettings();
  const extra = splitList(s.extraOutlookUrls);
  try {
    await chrome.scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: ['outlook-extra'] });
  } catch {
    /* none */
  }
  if (!extra.length) return;
  const granted = await chrome.permissions.contains({ origins: extra }).catch(() => false);
  if (!granted) return;
  await chrome.scripting.registerContentScripts([
    { id: 'outlook-extra', matches: extra, js: ['content/outlook.js'], runAt: 'document_idle', persistAcrossSessions: true },
  ]);
}

// ---------- messages ----------

const handlers = {
  engine: (m) => toEngine(m.payload),
  'settings.get': async () => {
    const s = await getSettings();
    return { processOnOpen: s.processOnOpen, autoScan: s.autoScan, autoScanOnlyWhenIdle: s.autoScanOnlyWhenIdle };
  },
  'items.known': async () => {
    const items = await getAll('items');
    return { ids: items.map((i) => i.id), convIds: items.map((i) => i.convId).filter(Boolean) };
  },
  'outlook.status': async () => {
    const tabs = await outlookTabs();
    if (!tabs.length) return { open: false };
    const res = await toOutlook({ type: 'ping' });
    return { open: true, active: !res.error, title: tabs[0].title || '', error: res.error };
  },
  'outlook.getOpen': () => toOutlook({ type: 'getOpenEmail' }),
  'outlook.insert': (m) => toOutlook({ type: 'insertDraft', text: m.text, convId: m.convId, itemId: m.itemId }),
  'outlook.scanNow': () => toOutlook({ type: 'scanNow' }),
  'outlook.indexListed': (m) => toOutlook({ type: 'indexListed', max: m.max || 300 }),
  'outlook.stopIndex': () => toOutlook({ type: 'stopIndexListed' }),
  'outlook.diagnose': () => toOutlook({ type: 'diagnose' }),
  badge: async (m) => {
    await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: '#b45309' });
    await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: m.text || '' });
    return { ok: true };
  },
  'scripts.sync': async () => {
    await syncExtraContentScripts();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  // NAS login typed into the file manager (only when config.js has no password). The engine can't read
  // chrome.storage itself, so it asks here.
  'nas.creds': async () => {
    try {
      const s = await chrome.storage.session.get(['username', 'password']);
      return s.username && s.password ? { username: s.username, password: s.password } : { none: true };
    } catch {
      return { none: true };
    }
  },
  // The NAS address and login saved from the setup form (when config.js is not filled in), for the engine.
  'nas.config': async () => {
    try {
      return (await chrome.storage.local.get('nasConfig')).nasConfig || { none: true };
    } catch {
      return { none: true };
    }
  },
  'app.open': async (m) => {
    await openApp(m.hash || '');
    return { ok: true };
  },
  'meeting.open': async () => {
    await openMeeting();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  'recorder.open': async () => {
    await openRecorder();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  'agent.open': async (m) => {
    const r = await openAgent({ task: m.task || '' });
    return { ok: true, started: !!r.started, busy: !!r.busy };
  },
  'page.read': () => readOpenPage(),
};

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg, _sender, sendResponse) => {
  if (!msg || msg.to !== 'bg') return false;
  const h = handlers[msg.type];
  if (!h) {
    sendResponse({ error: `unknown message ${msg.type}` });
    return false;
  }
  Promise.resolve()
    .then(() => h(msg))
    .then((res) => sendResponse(res ?? { ok: true }))
    .catch((err) => sendResponse({ error: err.message || String(err) }));
  return true;
});

async function setup() {
  await chrome.sidePanel.setPanelBehavior({ openPanelOnActionClick: true }).catch(() => {});
  await chrome.alarms.create('tick', { periodInMinutes: 1 });
  await syncExtraContentScripts().catch(() => {});
  await ensureEngine().catch(() => {});
}

chrome.runtime.onInstalled.addListener(setup);
chrome.runtime.onStartup.addListener(setup);

chrome.alarms.onAlarm.addListener((alarm) => {
  if (alarm.name === 'tick') toEngine({ type: 'tick' }).catch(() => {});
});

// Used by the automated tests.
globalThis.openApp = openApp;
globalThis.openMeeting = openMeeting;
globalThis.openRecorder = openRecorder;
globalThis.openAgent = openAgent;
globalThis.toEngine = toEngine;

===== FILE: extension/engine.js (390 lines) =====
// The engine: a hidden offscreen document that owns the long-running work — indexing the project folders,
// drafting email replies, Power Automate polling and the assistant chat. It is a normal window context, so
// it can use folder handles, DOMParser and pdf.js, and it is not stopped after 30 s idle the way the service
// worker is. (Mirroring itself runs in "NAS files", which has to stay open for big uploads anyway.)

import { getSettings, kvGet, kvSet, getAll, log } from './lib/db.js';
import { runIndex, applyDeepIndex } from './lib/indexer.js';
import { search, indexSummary, RECENCY_WORDS } from './lib/index.js';
import { getRootHandle, permissionState } from './lib/fsutil.js';
import { processEmail, regenerate, decideRequest, runItemAction, applyFolderResults, teachSource, listItems, summarize } from './lib/assistant.js';
import { pollFolderResponses } from './lib/automate.js';
import { listModels, listLocalModels, hasKey, activeModel, spentToday, spendReport, stopAllAI, resumeAI, pickGeminiFlash, generateText } from './lib/llm.js';
import { canCrossCheck, crossCheckMode } from './lib/crosscheck.js';
import { projectFolders } from './lib/folders.js';
import { nasClient, configProblem, writeTextFile } from './lib/nas.js';
import { sendChat, chatHistory, clearChat, runPendingTest } from './lib/chat.js';
import { listChanges, undoChange } from './lib/memory.js';
import { CONFIG, loadConfig } from './lib/config-store.js';
import { indexEmail } from './lib/mailindex.js';
import { invoiceReport, saveInvoiceReport, priorityList, setDone, addOwnTask } from './lib/todo.js';

const ui = (type, data) => chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ to: 'ui', type, ...data }).catch(() => {});
const bg = (type, data) => chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ to: 'bg', type, ...data }).catch(() => {});

// ---------- job runner: one file-system job at a time ----------

let job = null; // {name, started, controller, progress}
let indexAgain = false; // a reindex was asked for while another job ran

function startJob(name, fn) {
  if (job) {
    if (name === 'index') indexAgain = true;
    return { busy: true, job: job.name };
  }
  const controller = new AbortController();
  job = { name, started: Date.now(), controller, progress: null };
  const onProgress = (p) => {
    if (job) job.progress = p;
    ui('progress', { job: name, progress: p });
  };
  (async () => {
    try {
      const result = await fn({ signal: controller.signal, onProgress });
      ui('jobDone', { job: name, result: strip(result) });
    } catch (err) {
      await log('error', `${name}: ${err.message}`);
      ui('jobDone', { job: name, error: err.message });
    } finally {
      job = null;
      ui('status', {});
      if (indexAgain) {
        indexAgain = false;
        startJob('index', doIndex);
      }
    }
  })();
  return { started: true, job: name };
}

function strip(result) {
  if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return result;
  const { L, N, ...rest } = result;
  return rest;
}

// ---------- folders and index ----------

async function indexRoots() {
  const folders = (await projectFolders()).filter((f) => f.index);
  const granted = folders.filter((f) => f.perm === 'granted');
  if (!granted.length) {
    if (folders.length) throw new Error('Chrome needs your OK to read the project folders again — click "Reconnect folders" in the side panel.');
    throw new Error('No project folders yet. Add one with "Mirror a folder" (it is mirrored to the NAS and indexed here).');
  }
  return granted.map((f) => ({ root: f.handle, prefix: f.prefix, folderId: f.id, projectMode: f.projectMode }));
}

// The deep (OCR) index is built on the NAS by the HP (or a Pi) and fetched over WebDAV. Its size and date are
// checked first, so an unchanged index costs one small request instead of a download.
async function deepSource(settings, { force = false } = {}) {
  let stamp = null;
  return {
    get: async () => {
      const client = await nasClient();
      const st = await client.stat(settings.deepIndexFile);
      if (!st || st.isDir) return null;
      stamp = `${st.size}|${st.modified?.getTime() || 0}`;
      if (!force && (await kvGet('deepRemoteStamp')) === stamp) return { upToDate: true };
      return client.getBlob(settings.deepIndexFile);
    },
    done: async () => {
      if (stamp) await kvSet('deepRemoteStamp', stamp);
    },
    from: 'NAS',
  };
}

// Tells the HP which NAS folders are project folders, so its OCR job reads only those (never the media library).
async function publishProjectFolders() {
  const settings = await getSettings();
  const folders = (await projectFolders({ withPerm: false })).filter((f) => f.index).map((f) => f.nasPath).sort();
  const nasOnly = (settings.nasFolders || []).map((p) => `/${String(p).replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')}`).filter((p) => p !== '/').sort();
  const stamp = JSON.stringify({ folders, nasOnly });
  if ((await kvGet('projectFoldersPublished')) === stamp) return;
  const client = await nasClient();
  await writeTextFile(client, '/.assistant/project-folders.json', JSON.stringify({ updated: new Date().toISOString(), folders, nasOnly }, null, 2), 'application/json; charset=utf-8');
  await kvSet('projectFoldersPublished', stamp);
}

async function doIndex({ signal, onProgress }) {
  const settings = await getSettings();
  // NAS-only folders (standards copied from Drive, …) work without any local project folder.
  if ((settings.nasFolders || []).length && !(await projectFolders().catch(() => [])).some((f) => f.index && f.perm === 'granted')) {
    if (!configProblem()) await publishProjectFolders().catch((err) => log('error', `publishing project folders: ${err.message}`));
    return { files: (await getAll('files')).length, nasOnly: true, deep: await doDeep({ signal, onProgress, force: false, settings }) };
  }
  const roots = await indexRoots();
  if (!configProblem()) await publishProjectFolders().catch((err) => log('error', `publishing project folders: ${err.message}`));
  const status = await runIndex(roots, settings, { signal, onProgress, rootName: 'local' });
  if (!signal.aborted && !configProblem()) status.deep = await doDeep({ signal, onProgress, force: false, settings });
  return status;
}

async function doDeep({ signal, onProgress, force, settings }) {
  settings = settings || (await getSettings());
  const src = await deepSource(settings, { force });
  const res = await applyDeepIndex(src.get, settings, { signal, onProgress, force, from: src.from });
  if (res.found && !res.error && !signal?.aborted) await src.done();
  return res;
}

// ---------- email queue (one draft at a time) ----------

const queue = [];
let draining = false;
const onItem = (item) => {
  ui('item', { id: item.id, status: item.status });
updateBadge();
};

async function drain() {
  if (draining) return;
  draining = true;
  try {
    while (queue.length) {
      // Stop AI: emails not started yet wait in the queue until Resume AI.
      if ((await getSettings()).aiPaused) break;
      const { email, force } = queue.shift();
      try {
        await processEmail(email, { force, onChange: onItem });
      } catch (err) {
        await log('error', `process: ${err.message}`);
      }
    }
  } finally {
    draining = false;
  }
}

async function updateBadge() {
  const items = await listItems();
  const n = items.filter((i) => ['needs-info', 'needs-approval', 'ready'].includes(i.status)).length;
  bg('badge', { text: n ? String(n) : '' });
}

// ---------- chat (one reply at a time) ----------

let chatBusy = null; // AbortController of the reply being written

async function doChat(text) {
  if (chatBusy) return { busy: true };
  chatBusy = new AbortController();
  const signal = chatBusy.signal;
  (async () => {
    try {
      const reply = await sendChat(text, { signal, onUpdate: (m) => ui('chat', { message: m }) });
      if (reply.reindex) startJob('index', doIndex);
    } catch (err) {
      await log('error', `chat: ${err.message}`);
      ui('chat', { error: err.message });
    } finally {
      chatBusy = null;
    }
  })();
  return { started: true };
}

// ---------- timers ----------

let lastAutoIndex = 0;

async function tick() {
  const settings = await getSettings();
  try {
    const results = await pollFolderResponses();
    if (results.length) await applyFolderResults(results, { onChange: onItem });
  } catch (err) {
    await log('error', `bridge poll: ${err.message}`);
  }
  if (job) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  const idx = (await kvGet('indexStatus'))?.at || 0;
  if (settings.autoIndexMinutes > 0 && now - Math.max(idx, lastAutoIndex) > settings.autoIndexMinutes * 60000) {
    lastAutoIndex = now;
    try {
      await indexRoots();
      startJob('index', doIndex);
    } catch {
      /* folders not connected */
    }
  }
}

// ---------- status ----------

async function status() {
  const settings = await getSettings();
  const bridge = await getRootHandle('bridge');
  const items = await listItems();
  const folders = await projectFolders().catch(() => []);
  return {
    provider: settings.provider,
    model: activeModel(settings),
    hasKey: hasKey(settings),
    keys: { anthropic: !!settings.anthropicKey, gemini: !!settings.geminiKey },
    folders: {
      bridge: bridge ? { name: bridge.name, perm: await permissionState(bridge) } : null,
    },
    projectFolders: folders.map((f) => ({ id: f.id, name: f.name, nasPath: f.nasPath, perm: f.perm, index: f.index, projectMode: f.projectMode, lastSync: f.lastSync })),
    nas: { configured: !configProblem(), problem: configProblem(), url: CONFIG.serverUrl },
    index: await indexSummary(),
    deep: await kvGet('deepApplied'),
    job: job ? { name: job.name, started: job.started, progress: job.progress } : null,
    queue: queue.length + (draining ? 1 : 0),
    ai: {
      paused: !!settings.aiPaused,
      spentToday: await spentToday(),
      spend: await spendReport(),
      dailyBudgetUsd: Number(settings.dailyBudgetUsd) || 0,
      crossCheck: canCrossCheck(settings),
      crossCheckMode: crossCheckMode(settings),
      crossCheckOff: settings.crossCheck === false,
    },
    chatBusy: !!chatBusy,
    pendingActions: ((await kvGet('pendingActions')) || []).length,
    summary: summarize(items),
    actions: (settings.actions || []).filter((a) => a.enabled !== false).map((a) => ({ name: a.name, kind: a.kind || 'read', description: a.description || '', params: a.params || '' })),
  };
}

// ---------- message handling ----------

const handlers = {
  ping: async () => ({ ok: true }),
  // The kill switch: cancels every Claude/Gemini call in progress (here, in the meeting window, anywhere) and
  // refuses new ones until Resume.
  'ai.stop': async () => {
    await stopAllAI();
    ui('aiState', { paused: true });
    return { paused: true };
  },
  'ai.resume': async () => {
    await resumeAI();
    ui('aiState', { paused: false });
    drain();
    return { paused: false };
  },
  status,
  'index.run': async () => startJob('index', doIndex),
  'deep.apply': async (m) => startJob('deep', (o) => doDeep({ ...o, force: !!m.force })),
  'job.stop': async () => {
    if (job) job.controller.abort();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  // Local search — no tokens. Meeting prompts, the chat and the Files tab all use it.
  search: async (m) => ({
    hits: await search(m.q || '', {
      k: m.k || 12,
      charBudget: m.charBudget || 20000,
      perFile: m.perFile || 2,
      maxChunkChars: m.maxChunkChars || 400,
      boostPrefixes: m.projects || [],
      preferRecent: m.preferRecent ?? RECENCY_WORDS.test(m.q || ''),
      excludePaths: m.excludePaths ? new Set(m.excludePaths) : null,
    }),
  }),
  'nas.test': async () => {
    try {
      const client = await nasClient();
      const entries = await client.list('/');
      return { ok: true, entries: entries.length };
    } catch (err) {
      return { ok: false, error: err.message };
    }
  },
  'email.capture': async (m) => {
    if (!m.email) return { error: 'no email' };
    queue.push({ email: m.email, force: !!m.force });
    drain();
    return { queued: true };
  },
  'item.regenerate': async (m) => regenerate(m.id, { notes: m.notes, effort: m.effort, onChange: onItem }),
  'item.reprocess': async (m) => {
    const item = (await getAll('items')).find((i) => i.id === m.id);
    if (!item) return { error: 'not found' };
    queue.push({ email: { ...item, body: item.bodyRaw || item.bodyClean }, force: true });
    drain();
    return { queued: true };
  },
  'item.decide': async (m) => decideRequest(m.id, m.key, m.approve, { params: m.params, onChange: onItem }),
  'item.action': async (m) => runItemAction(m.id, m.action, m.params || {}, { onChange: onItem }),
  'item.teach': async (m) => teachSource(m.id, m.path, m.kind, { onChange: onItem }),
  // To do: the priority list and the invoice summary.
  'todo.priorities': async () => priorityList(),
  'todo.last': async () => (await kvGet('priorities:last')) || null,
  'todo.done': async (m) => setDone(m.id, !!m.done),
  'todo.addTask': async (m) => addOwnTask(m.text, m.due || ''),
  'todo.invoices': async (m) => {
    const rep = await invoiceReport(m.month);
    if (rep.invoices.length) {
      rep.savedTo = await saveInvoiceReport(rep).catch((err) => `not saved (${err.message})`);
      await kvSet(`invoices:${rep.month}`, rep);
    }
    return rep;
  },
  // "Index these emails" in Outlook on the web: one email at a time, no AI.
  'mail.index': async (m) => indexEmail(m.email),
  'llm.models': async (m) => {
    const settings = { ...(await getSettings()), ...(m.override || {}) };
    const provider = m.provider || settings.provider;
    if (provider === 'local') return { models: await listLocalModels(settings), auto: '' };
    const models = await listModels(settings, provider);
    return { models, auto: provider === 'gemini' ? pickGeminiFlash(models) || '' : '' };
  },
  // Settings → "Test web search": one small search with the chosen model.
  'llm.webtest': async (m) => {
    const settings = await getSettings();
    const provider = m.provider || settings.provider;
    const r = await generateText({ ...settings, _tag: 'test' }, { provider, web: true, effort: 'low', maxTokens: 300, system: 'Answer in one short sentence.', prompt: 'Search the web: what is the latest stable version of Google Chrome?' });
    return { model: r.model, sources: r.sources.length, text: r.text.trim().slice(0, 140) };
  },
  'chat.send': async (m) => doChat(m.text || ''),
  'chat.stop': async () => {
    chatBusy?.abort();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  'chat.history': async () => ({ messages: await chatHistory(), busy: !!chatBusy }),
  'chat.clear': async () => {
    await clearChat();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  'chat.runTest': async (m) => runPendingTest(m.messageId, m.testId),
  'changes.list': async () => ({ changes: await listChanges(40) }),
  'changes.undo': async (m) => {
    const rec = await undoChange(m.id);
    if (/^set_folder_options$/.test(rec.tool)) startJob('index', doIndex);
    return { ok: true, change: rec };
  },
  tick: async () => {
    await tick();
    return { ok: true };
  },
  badge: async () => {
    await updateBadge();
    return { ok: true };
  },
};

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg, _sender, sendResponse) => {
  if (!msg || msg.to !== 'engine') return false;
  const h = handlers[msg.type];
  if (!h) {
    sendResponse({ error: `unknown engine message ${msg.type}` });
    return false;
  }
  Promise.resolve()
    .then(() => h(msg))
    .then((res) => sendResponse(res ?? { ok: true }))
    .catch((err) => sendResponse({ error: err.message || String(err), kind: err.kind }));
  return true;
});

// Resume AI (from any page): drafts that waited in the queue go ahead.
chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg) => {
  if (msg?.type === 'ai.resumed') drain();
  return false;
});

await loadConfig();
updateBadge();
