    : `✓ Web search works with ${esc(res.model)}: ${res.sources} source${res.sources === 1 ? '' : 's'} found (“${esc(res.text)}”).`;
}));

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- folders

async function renderFolders() {
  const h = await getRootHandle('bridge');
  const el = $('f-bridge');
  if (!h) {
    el.innerHTML = '<span class="pill">not set</span>';
  } else {
    const p = await permissionState(h);
    el.innerHTML = `<b>${esc(h.name)}</b> ${p === 'granted' ? '<span class="pill ok">connected</span>' : '<button type="button" data-grant="bridge">Grant access</button>'}`;
  }
  await renderFolderList();
}

async function renderFolderList() {
  const folders = await projectFolders();
  if (!folders.length) {
    $('folderList').innerHTML = '<p class="small muted">No project folders yet. Click "Mirror a folder…", choose the folder (or the drive folder) that holds your projects, and pick where it goes on the NAS.</p>';
    return;
  }
  $('folderList').innerHTML = folders.map((f) => {
    const state = f.perm === 'granted' ? '<span class="pill ok">connected</span>' : f.perm === 'missing' ? '<span class="pill bad">folder lost — mirror it again</span>' : `<button type="button" data-grant-folder="${esc(f.id)}">Grant access</button>`;
    return `<div class="action-card" data-folder="${esc(f.id)}">
      <div class="row"><b class="grow">📁 ${esc(f.name)} → ${esc(f.nasPath)}</b>${state}</div>
      <div class="row">
        <label class="check small"><input type="checkbox" data-pref="index" ${f.index ? 'checked' : ''}> Index this folder</label>
        <label class="field small" style="min-width:260px"><span>Projects in this folder</span>
          <select data-pref="projectMode">
            <option value="subfolders" ${f.projectMode !== 'single' ? 'selected' : ''}>Each subfolder is a project</option>
            <option value="single" ${f.projectMode === 'single' ? 'selected' : ''}>The whole folder is one project</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

async function renderNas() {
  await loadConfig({ fresh: true });
  const url = CONFIG.serverUrl && !/your-nas\.example/.test(CONFIG.serverUrl) ? CONFIG.serverUrl : '';
  $('nasInfo').innerHTML = !url
    ? 'Not set up yet. Click below (or fill in <code>config.js</code> in the extension folder).'
    : CONFIG.source === 'config.js'
      ? `<b>${esc(url)}</b> as <b>${esc(CONFIG.username || '?')}</b> — built into <code>config.js</code> (change it there).`
      : `<b>${esc(url)}</b> as <b>${esc(CONFIG.username || '?')}</b> — saved on this computer${CONFIG.password ? ' with the password' : '; the password is asked once per browser session'}.`;
  $('nasChange').hidden = CONFIG.source === 'config.js' && !!url;
  $('nasForget').hidden = CONFIG.source !== 'settings';
}
$('nasChange').addEventListener('click', () => bg('app.open', { hash: '#nas-setup' }));
$('nasForget').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!confirm('Forget the NAS address and login on this computer? Your files are not touched.')) return;
  await forgetSavedConfig();
  $('nasOut').textContent = 'Forgotten.';
  renderNas();
});

$('addFolder').addEventListener('click', () => bg('app.open', { hash: '#new-mirror' }));

$('saveNasFolders').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const list = $('nasFolders').value.split(/[\n,]/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean).map((x) => `/${x.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')}`).filter((x) => x !== '/');
  settings = await saveSettings({ nasFolders: [...new Set(list)] });
  $('nasFolders').value = settings.nasFolders.join('\n');
  $('nasFoldersOut').textContent = list.length ? 'Saved. The HP reads them on its next run (every 2 hours), then they appear in search.' : 'Saved.';
  engine('index.run');
});
$('openNasFiles').addEventListener('click', () => bg('app.open', { hash: '' }));

$('folderList').addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
  const key = e.target.dataset.pref;
  const card = e.target.closest('[data-folder]');
  if (!key || !card) return;
  const prefs = { ...(settings.folderPrefs || {}) };
  const cur = { ...(prefs[card.dataset.folder] || {}) };
  cur[key] = e.target.type === 'checkbox' ? e.target.checked : e.target.value;
  prefs[card.dataset.folder] = cur;
  settings = await saveSettings({ folderPrefs: prefs });
  flashSaved();
  engine('index.run');
});

$('folderList').addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
  const g = e.target.closest('[data-grant-folder]');
  if (!g) return;
  const f = (await projectFolders({ withPerm: false })).find((x) => x.id === g.dataset.grantFolder);
  if (f) await requestPermission(f.handle, 'read');
  await renderFolderList();
  engine('index.run');
});

document.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
  const pick = e.target.closest('[data-pick]');
  if (pick) {
    const which = pick.dataset.pick;
    try {
      const h = await window.showDirectoryPicker({ id: `mm-${which}`, mode: 'readwrite' });
      await setRootHandle(which, h);
      toast(`${which} folder set to ${h.name}`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.name !== 'AbortError') toast(err.message, 6000);
    }
    renderFolders();
    return;
  }
  const grant = e.target.closest('[data-grant]');
  if (grant) {
    const h = await getRootHandle(grant.dataset.grant);
    await requestPermission(h);
    renderFolders();
  }
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- Outlook URLs

$('grantOutlook').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const origins = splitList($('grantOutlook').closest('section').querySelector('[data-key="extraOutlookUrls"]').value);
  if (!origins.length) { $('grantOut').textContent = 'Add an address first.'; return; }
  try {
    const ok = await chrome.permissions.request({ origins });
    if (ok) await bg('scripts.sync');
    $('grantOut').textContent = ok ? 'Allowed. Reload that Outlook tab.' : 'Not allowed.';
  } catch (err) {
    $('grantOut').textContent = err.message;
  }
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- Power Automate actions

const ACTION_DEFAULTS = { name: '', description: '', kind: 'read', transport: 'http', url: '', params: '', autoRun: false, auto: false, timeoutSec: 300, enabled: true };

function actionCard(a, i) {
  return `<div class="action-card stack" data-i="${i}">
    <div class="grid3">
      <label class="field"><span>Name (short, no spaces)</span><input type="text" data-a="name" value="${esc(a.name)}" placeholder="sap_po_status"></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Type</span><select data-a="kind"><option value="read" ${a.kind !== 'write' ? 'selected' : ''}>Read — fetches data</option><option value="write" ${a.kind === 'write' ? 'selected' : ''}>Write — changes records</option></select></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Connection</span><select data-a="transport"><option value="http" ${a.transport !== 'folder' ? 'selected' : ''}>HTTP trigger URL</option><option value="folder" ${a.transport === 'folder' ? 'selected' : ''}>Bridge folder (OneDrive)</option></select></label>
    </div>
    <label class="field" ${a.transport === 'folder' ? 'hidden' : ''}><span>Flow URL (from "When an HTTP request is received")</span>
      <div class="row nowrap"><input type="url" class="grow" data-a="url" value="${esc(a.url)}" placeholder="https://prod-00.canadacentral.logic.azure.com/workflows/…"><button type="button" data-allow="${i}" hidden>Allow this address</button></div></label>
    <label class="field"><span>What it does / when to use it (the AI reads this)</span><textarea data-a="description" rows="2" placeholder="Returns SAP purchase-order status, amounts and vendor for a PO number. Use when an email asks about a PO, invoice or payment.">${esc(a.description)}</textarea></label>
    <label class="field"><span>Parameters — comma separated; "name: hint" or "name={{subject}}" to prefill from the email ({{subject}} {{from}} {{project}} {{summary}} {{status}} {{missing}} {{draft}} {{date}})</span><input type="text" data-a="params" value="${esc(a.params)}" placeholder="po_number: 10-digit SAP PO, project={{project}}"></label>
    <div class="row">
      <label class="check small" ${a.kind === 'write' ? 'hidden' : ''}><input type="checkbox" data-a="autoRun" ${a.autoRun ? 'checked' : ''}> Let the AI run this without asking</label>
      <label class="check small" ${a.kind === 'write' ? '' : 'hidden'}><input type="checkbox" data-a="auto" ${a.auto ? 'checked' : ''}> Run after every draft without asking (e.g. tracker log)</label>
      <label class="check small"><input type="checkbox" data-a="enabled" ${a.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Enabled</label>
      <label class="field small" style="width:150px"><span>Timeout (s)</span><input type="number" min="10" data-a="timeoutSec" value="${esc(a.timeoutSec ?? 300)}"></label>
      <span class="grow"></span>
      <button type="button" class="danger" data-remove="${i}">Remove</button>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

function originPattern(url) {
  try { return `${new URL(url).origin}/*`; } catch { return null; }
}

// Shows "Allow this address" for flow URLs the extension has no access to yet.
async function checkActionAccess() {
  for (const b of document.querySelectorAll('[data-allow]')) {
    const a = settings.actions[Number(b.dataset.allow)];
    const pattern = a && a.transport !== 'folder' ? originPattern(a.url) : null;
    b.hidden = !pattern || await chrome.permissions.contains({ origins: [pattern] }).catch(() => true);
  }
}

function renderActions() {
  const acts = settings.actions || [];
  $('actions').innerHTML = acts.length ? acts.map(actionCard).join('') : '<p class="small muted">No flows yet.</p>';
  checkActionAccess();
}

async function saveActions(rerender = false) {
  settings = await saveSettings({ actions: settings.actions });
  flashSaved();
  if (rerender) renderActions();
}

$('actions').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
  const card = e.target.closest('.action-card');
  const field = e.target.dataset.a;
  if (!card || !field) return;
  const a = settings.actions[Number(card.dataset.i)];
  a[field] = e.target.type === 'checkbox' ? e.target.checked : field === 'timeoutSec' ? Number(e.target.value) || 300 : e.target.value;
  if (field === 'name') a.name = a.name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
  clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = setTimeout(() => saveActions(field === 'kind' || field === 'transport'), 300);
});
$('actions').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  if (e.target.dataset.a === 'kind' || e.target.dataset.a === 'transport' || e.target.type === 'checkbox') {
    const card = e.target.closest('.action-card');
    const a = settings.actions[Number(card.dataset.i)];
    a[e.target.dataset.a] = e.target.type === 'checkbox' ? e.target.checked : e.target.value;
    saveActions(true);
  }
});
$('actions').addEventListener('focusout', (e) => { if (e.target.dataset.a === 'url') checkActionAccess(); });
$('actions').addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
  const allow = e.target.closest('[data-allow]');
  if (allow) {
    const pattern = originPattern(settings.actions[Number(allow.dataset.allow)].url);
    const ok = pattern && await chrome.permissions.request({ origins: [pattern] }).catch(() => false);
    toast(ok ? 'Access allowed.' : 'Access not granted — the flow cannot be called.');
    checkActionAccess();
    return;
  }
  const r = e.target.closest('[data-remove]');
  if (!r) return;
  if (!confirm('Remove this flow from the assistant? (The flow itself is not touched.)')) return;
  settings.actions.splice(Number(r.dataset.remove), 1);
  saveActions(true);
});
$('addAction').addEventListener('click', () => {
  settings.actions = [...(settings.actions || []), { ...ACTION_DEFAULTS }];
  saveActions(true);
});
$('exportActions').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(settings.actions || [], null, 2));
  toast('Flow list copied as JSON.');
});
$('importActions').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const text = prompt('Paste a JSON array of flows (as produced by "Copy as JSON"):');
  if (!text) return;
  try {
    const arr = JSON.parse(text);
    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) throw new Error('expected a JSON array');
    settings.actions = arr.map((a) => ({ ...ACTION_DEFAULTS, ...a }));
    await saveActions(true);
    toast(`Imported ${arr.length} flow(s).`);
  } catch (err) {
    toast(`Could not import: ${err.message}`, 6000);
  }
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- teaching

async function renderRules() {
  const rules = await getRules();
  $('rules').innerHTML = rules.length
    ? rules.slice().reverse().map((r) => `<div class="rule"><span class="pill ${r.kind === 'prefer' ? 'ok' : 'bad'}">${r.kind === 'prefer' ? 'use' : 'skip'}</span>
        <span class="grow" title="${esc(r.terms.join(', '))}"><b>${esc(r.path)}</b> <span class="muted">for: ${esc(r.terms.slice(0, 8).join(', '))}${r.sender ? ` · from ${esc(r.sender)}` : ''}</span></span>
        <button type="button" data-del-rule="${esc(r.id)}">Forget</button></div>`).join('')
    : '<p class="small muted">Nothing learned yet.</p>';
}

$('rules').addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest('[data-del-rule]');
  if (!b) return;
  await deleteRule(b.dataset.delRule);
  renderRules();
});

let hintTimer = null;
$('hintPath').addEventListener('input', () => {
  clearTimeout(hintTimer);
  hintTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
    const q = $('hintPath').value.trim();
    if (q.length < 2) return;
    const res = await engine('search', { q, k: 15 });
    const paths = [...new Set((res?.hits || []).map((h) => h.path))];
    $('hintPaths').innerHTML = paths.map((p) => `<option value="${esc(p)}"></option>`).join('');
  }, 250);
});

$('addHint').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const text = $('hintText').value.trim();
  const path = $('hintPath').value.trim();
  if (!(await get('files', path))) { $('hintOut').textContent = 'Pick a file from the suggestions (it must be in the index).'; return; }
  try {
    await addHint(text, path);
    $('hintOut').textContent = 'Hint added.';
    $('hintText').value = '';
    $('hintPath').value = '';
    renderRules();
  } catch (err) {
    $('hintOut').textContent = err.message;
  }
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- data

$('rebuildIndex').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!confirm('Throw away the index and read every file again? Your files are not touched. Indexed Outlook emails and saved meetings are kept.')) return;
  const { resetIndex } = await import('./lib/index.js');
  await resetIndex();
  for (const k of ['indexStatus', 'deepApplied']) await kvSet(k, null);
  const res = await engine('index.run');
  toast(res?.error || 'Rebuilding the index…');
});
$('clearDrafts').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!confirm('Clear the draft log? Outlook is not touched.')) return;
  await clear('items');
  engine('badge');
  toast('Draft log cleared.');
});
$('clearRules').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!confirm('Forget everything the assistant has been taught about documents?')) return;
  await kvSet('rules', []);
  renderRules();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- standing instructions

const SCOPE_LABEL = { all: 'everything', email: 'email drafts', meeting: 'meetings', chat: 'the chat' };

async function renderInstructions() {
  const list = await listInstructions();
  $('instList').innerHTML = list.length
    ? list.slice().reverse().map((i) => `<div class="rule"><span class="pill">${esc(SCOPE_LABEL[i.scope] || i.scope)}</span><span class="grow">${esc(i.text)}</span><button type="button" data-del-inst="${esc(i.id)}">Remove</button></div>`).join('')
    : '<p class="small muted">None yet.</p>';
}

$('addInst').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const text = $('instText').value.trim();
  if (!text) return;
  await addInstruction(text, $('instScope').value);
  $('instText').value = '';
  settings = await getSettings();
  renderInstructions();
  flashSaved();
});
$('instList').addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
  const b = e.target.closest('[data-del-inst]');
  if (!b) return;
  await removeInstruction(b.dataset.delInst);
  settings = await getSettings();
  renderInstructions();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- export / import (moving to another computer)

const SECRET = new Set(['anthropicKey', 'geminiKey', 'localKey', 'hpCommandKey']);

$('exportSetup').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const s = await getSettings();
  // Only known settings, never keys or anything else stored alongside them.
  const out = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(s).filter(([k]) => k in DEFAULT_SETTINGS && !SECRET.has(k) && k !== 'folderPrefs'));
  out.rules = await getRules();
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify({ projectAssistantSettings: 1, at: new Date().toISOString(), settings: out }, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' }));
  a.download = `project-assistant-settings-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
  a.click();
});

$('importSetup').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const inp = document.createElement('input');
  inp.type = 'file';
  inp.accept = 'application/json,.json';
  inp.onchange = async () => {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(await inp.files[0].text());
      const incoming = data.settings || data;
      const patch = {};
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(incoming)) if (k in DEFAULT_SETTINGS && !SECRET.has(k) && k !== 'folderPrefs') patch[k] = v;
      settings = await saveSettings(patch);
      if (Array.isArray(incoming.rules)) await kvSet('rules', incoming.rules);
      fillFields();
      renderActions();
      renderRules();
      renderInstructions();
      toast(`Imported ${Object.keys(patch).length} settings.`);
    } catch (err) {
      toast(`Could not import: ${err.message}`, 6000);
    }
  };
  inp.click();
});

  settings = await getSettings();
  fillFields();
  renderNas();
  renderFolders();
  renderActions();
  renderRules();
  renderInstructions();
  renderAccuracy();
  renderSpend();
  renderLocalFor();

  renderProjectMail();
  renderModelPicker('gemini');
  $('nasFolders').value = (settings.nasFolders || []).join('\n');
  if (location.hash) {
    let id = location.hash.slice(1);
    try { id = decodeURIComponent(id); } catch { /* keep it as is */ }
    document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView();
  }
})();

(async () => {
  const sel = $('ttsVoice');
  const gem = $('ttsGeminiVoice');
  const s = await getSettings();
  gem.innerHTML = GEMINI_VOICES.map((v) => `<option value="${v}">${v}</option>`).join('');
  gem.value = s.ttsGeminiVoice || 'Kore';
  const voices = await computerVoices();
  for (const v of voices) {
    const o = document.createElement('option');
    o.value = v.name;
    o.textContent = `${v.name} (${v.lang})${v.localService ? '' : ' — online'}`;
    sel.append(o);
  }
  sel.value = s.ttsVoice || '';
})();

});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- run on the HP

$('hpTest').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  $('hpOut').textContent = 'Asking the HP…';
  try {
    const { runOnHp } = await import('./lib/hp.js');
    const r = await runOnHp('echo "$(whoami)@$(hostname) in $(pwd)"', { timeout: 15 });
    $('hpOut').textContent = r.exit === 0 ? `✓ ${r.stdout.trim()}` : `exit ${r.exit}: ${(r.stderr || r.stdout).trim()}`;
  } catch (err) {
    $('hpOut').textContent = err.message;
  }
});

===== FILE: extension/meeting.css (67 lines) =====
body { margin: 0; }
.m-top { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 10; background: var(--bg); padding: 10px 12px 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }
.brand { font-size: 15px; margin: 0; }
.wrap { padding: 10px 12px 16px; }
.nowrap { flex-wrap: nowrap; }
#sources .row button { flex: 1 1 0; }

.meters { display: grid; gap: 6px; }
.meter { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 62px 1fr 96px; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12px; }
.meter .lbl { font-weight: 600; }
.meter .bar { position: relative; height: 12px; background: var(--surface-2); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; overflow: hidden; }

===== FILE: extension/meeting.js (1213 lines) =====
// Meeting window: listens to the Teams meeting (desktop app via system audio, or a Chrome tab) and the user's
// microphone, shows both sound levels, transcribes (free on this computer, or Gemini), flashes prompts of what
// to say with quotes from the project files, answers private typed questions, and saves the transcript to
// the NAS (every minute, and at the end with a summary).

import { getSettings, saveSettings, put, kvGet } from './lib/db.js';
import { createAudioContext, captureTeamsApp, captureTeamsTab, captureMicrophone, AudioChannel } from './lib/audio.js';
import { Transcriber } from './lib/stt.js';
import { makePrompt, answerQuestion, summarize, shouldPrompt, checkResult, researchTopic } from './lib/meeting-ai.js';
import { docx } from './lib/docx.js';
import { detectProjects } from './lib/projects.js';
import { addHint } from './lib/teach.js';
import { hasKey, pickProvider, stopAllAI, resumeAI, listModels, pickGeminiFlash } from './lib/llm.js';
import { modelChoices, fillModelSelect } from './lib/models.js';
import { localAI } from './lib/localai.js';
import { nasClient, writeTextFile, safeFileName, ensureDir } from './lib/nas.js';
import { after } from './lib/util.js';
import { projectFolders } from './lib/folders.js';
import { pageLabel, chunkText } from './lib/text.js';
import { IndexWriter } from './lib/index.js';
import { Recording } from './lib/recorder.js';
import { speak, stopSpeaking, attachDictation } from './lib/voice.js';

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const hms = (d) => `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
const money = (c) => (c < 0.01 ? `$${c.toFixed(4)}` : `$${c.toFixed(2)}`);

const state = {
  settings: null,
  ctx: null,
  them: null, // AudioChannel for the meeting
  me: null, // AudioChannel for the microphone
  themKind: '',
  stt: null,
  listening: false,
  meetingId: '',
  startedAt: 0,
  entries: [], // {who, text, start, end}
  prompts: [], // cards
  answers: [],
  project: '', // chosen by the user
  autoProjects: [],
  lastPromptAt: 0,
  lastPromptIdx: 0,
  prompting: null, // AbortController
  cost: 0,
  errors: [],
  savedPath: '',
  savedAt: 0,
  summary: null,
  pip: null,
  mirrorPaths: [], // NAS paths of the mirrored project folders
  recording: null, // a local audio recording of the meeting (Record audio toggle)
  aiPaused: false, // Stop AI (here or in the side panel): no prompts, answers or Gemini calls until resumed
  // Tests can provide audio instead of the real screen-share / microphone pickers.
  captureOverride: null, // async (kind: 'app'|'tab') => MediaStream
  micOverride: null, // async () => MediaStream
};
window.meeting = state;

let toastTimer = null;
function toast(msg, ms = 4000) {
  const t = $('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.hidden = false;
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    t.hidden = true;
  }, ms);
}

function setStatus(text) {
  $('status').textContent = text;
}

function warn(html) {
  $('warn').innerHTML = html || '';
  $('warn').hidden = !html;
}

function myLabel() {
  return state.settings?.myVoiceLabel || 'Me';
}

function projects() {
  return state.project ? [state.project] : state.autoProjects;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- listening

async function ensureContext() {
  if (!state.ctx) state.ctx = await createAudioContext();
  if (state.ctx.state === 'suspended') await state.ctx.resume();
}

async function listen(kind) {
  if (state.stopping) {
    toast('Still saving the last meeting — one moment.');
    return;
  }
  state.settings = await getSettings();
  try {
    await ensureContext();
  } catch (err) {
    warn(`Couldn't start audio processing: ${esc(err.message)}`);
    return;
  }
  let stream;
  try {
    setStatus(kind === 'app' ? 'Choose "Entire screen" and tick "Also share system audio"…' : 'Choose the Teams tab and keep "Also share tab audio" on…');
    stream = state.captureOverride ? await state.captureOverride(kind) : kind === 'app' ? await captureTeamsApp() : await captureTeamsTab();
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === 'NotAllowedError' || err.name === 'AbortError') {
      setStatus(state.listening ? 'Still listening to the previous source.' : 'Not listening — sharing was cancelled.');
      return;
    }
    warn(esc(err.message || String(err)));
    setStatus(state.listening ? 'Still listening to the previous source.' : 'Not listening.');
    return;
  }
  if (state.recording) await stopRecording('The meeting source changed, so the recording was saved. Click Record audio to start a new one.');
  state.them?.stop();
  state.them = new AudioChannel(state.ctx, stream, 'Them');
  state.themKind = kind;
  state.them.addEventListener('segment', (e) => onSegment(e, 'Them'));
  state.them.addEventListener('ended', () => {
    if (state.listening) {
      warn('Teams sharing stopped. Click <b>Teams app</b> or <b>Teams in Chrome</b> to listen again, or <b>Stop and save</b>.');
      setStatus('Meeting sound stopped.');
    }
  });
  warn('');
  if ($('chkMic').checked && !state.me) await startMic();
  await startSession();
  setStatus(`Listening to ${kind === 'app' ? 'the Teams app' : 'Teams in Chrome'}${state.me ? ' and your microphone' : ''}.`);
}

async function startMic() {
  try {
    const stream = state.micOverride ? await state.micOverride() : await captureMicrophone();
    state.me = new AudioChannel(state.ctx, stream, myLabel());
    state.me.addEventListener('segment', (e) => onSegment(e, myLabel()));
    state.me.addEventListener('ended', () => {
      state.me = null;
    });
  } catch (err) {
    toast(`Microphone not available (${err.message}). Listening to the meeting only.`, 6000);
    $('chkMic').checked = false;
  }
}

function stopMic() {
  state.me?.stop();
  state.me = null;
}

async function startSession() {
  if (state.listening) return;
  state.listening = true;
  state.meetingId = `m-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;
  state.startedAt = Date.now();
  state.entries = [];
  state.prompts = [];
  state.answers = [];
  state.summary = null;
  state.minutes = null;
  state.minutesFinal = false;
  state.minutesBase = '';
  state.savedPath = '';
  state.cost = 0;
  state.errors = [];
  state.lastPromptAt = 0;
  state.lastPromptIdx = 0;
  $('btnStop').disabled = false;
  $('clock').hidden = false;
  renderTranscript();
  renderPrompts();
  renderAnswers();
  state.stt = new Transcriber(state.settings);
  state.stt.addEventListener('progress', (e) => {
    if (e.total) setStatus(`Getting the free speech model ready (first time only): ${Math.round((100 * e.loaded) / e.total)}% of ${e.file.split('/').pop()}`);
  });
  state.stt.addEventListener('text', (e) => onText(e.segment, e.text, e));
  state.stt.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
    const msg = e.error?.message || String(e.error);
    state.errors.push(msg);
    if (e.fatal) {
      warn(`The speech model couldn't load: ${esc(msg)}. <button id="sttRetry" type="button">Try again</button>${state.settings.geminiKey ? ' or switch Transcription to Gemini in ⚙.' : ''}`);
      $('sttRetry')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        warn('');
        state.stt.retry();
      });
    } else if (state.errors.length <= 3) toast(`Transcription: ${msg}`, 6000);
  });
  state.stt.start().then(
    () => {
      state.sttEngine = state.stt?.engine;
      if (state.listening) setStatus(`Listening${{ gemini: ' · transcribing with Gemini', both: ' · transcribing with Gemini, checked by the free model' }[state.stt.engine] || ' · transcribing on this computer (free)'}.`);
    },
    () => {},
  );
  scheduleAutosave();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- local recording of the meeting's sound

async function toggleRecording() {
  if (state.recording) {
    await stopRecording();
    return;
  }
  if (!state.them && !state.me) {
    toast('Start listening first (Teams app or Teams in Chrome) — the recording uses the same sound.');
    return;
  }
  const rec = new Recording({ kind: 'audio', streams: [state.them?.stream, state.me?.stream].filter(Boolean), label: 'meeting' });
  try {
    await rec.start();
  } catch (err) {
    toast(`Couldn't record: ${err.message}`, 6000);
    return;
  }
  state.recording = rec;
  renderRecording();
}

async function stopRecording(note) {
  const rec = state.recording;
  if (!rec) return null;
  state.recording = null;
  renderRecording();
  const r = await rec.stop();
  toast(note || `Recording saved: ${r.where}`, 6000);
  state.recordedTo = r.where;
  return r;
}

function renderRecording() {
  const b = $('btnRec');
  const rec = state.recording;
  b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!!rec));
  b.textContent = rec ? `■ Recording ${Math.floor(rec.seconds / 60)}:${pad(Math.floor(rec.seconds % 60))}` : '⏺ Record audio';
}

async function stopAndSave() {
  if (!state.listening || state.stopping) return;
  state.stopping = true;
  $('btnStop').disabled = true;
  // No new meeting can start until this one is saved.
  $('btnApp').disabled = true;
  $('btnTab').disabled = true;
  const stt = state.stt;
  try {
    await finishMeeting(stt);
  } finally {
    state.stopping = false;
    $('btnApp').disabled = false;
    $('btnTab').disabled = false;
  }
}

async function finishMeeting(stt) {
  if (state.recording) await stopRecording();
  state.them?.stop();
  stopMic();
  state.them = null;
  setStatus('Finishing the transcript…');
  // Let the last segments be transcribed (up to a minute).
  const t0 = Date.now();
  while (state.stt && (state.stt.queue.length || state.stt.busy) && Date.now() - t0 < 60000) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
  state.prompting?.abort();
  state.listening = false;
  await detectProject({ now: true });
  if (state.settings.meetingSummary && state.entries.length) {
    setStatus('Writing the summary…');
    try {
      state.summary = await summarize(state.entries, { projects: projects() });
      if (state.summary?.cost) state.cost += state.summary.cost;
    } catch (err) {
      state.errors.push(`summary: ${err.message}`);
      toast(`Summary failed: ${err.message}`, 6000);
    }
  }
  await save(true);
  if (state.summary) await makeMinutes({ final: true });
  stt?.stop();
  if (state.stt === stt) state.stt = null;
  $('clock').hidden = true;
  setStatus(state.savedPath ? `Saved to the NAS: ${state.savedPath}` : 'Stopped. (Not saved to the NAS — see the message below.)');
  renderSummary();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- transcript

function onSegment(e, who) {
  if (!state.stt) return;
  state.stt.push({ samples: e.samples, start: e.start.getTime(), end: e.end.getTime(), who });
  updateBehind();
}

function words(t) {
  return String(t || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter((w) => w.length > 1);
}

function similar(a, b) {
  const A = new Set(words(a));
  const B = words(b);
  if (!A.size || !B.length) return 0;
  return B.filter((w) => A.has(w)).length / Math.max(A.size, B.length);
}

// With speakers (no headset) the meeting also reaches the microphone. Drop those copies.
function isEcho(entry) {
  const others = state.entries.filter((x) => x.who !== entry.who && Math.abs(x.start - entry.start) < 6000);
  return others.some((x) => similar(x.text, entry.text) >= 0.6);
}

function onText(seg, text, extra = {}) {
  updateBehind();
  const t = String(text || '').trim();
  if (!t) return;
  const entry = { who: seg.who, text: t, start: seg.start, end: seg.end };
  // "both": the two transcriptions heard different numbers — shown so the user knows which line to doubt.
  if (extra.unsure && extra.alt) Object.assign(entry, { unsure: true, alt: String(extra.alt).trim() });
  if (isEcho(entry)) {
    if (entry.who === 'Them') {
      // The meeting copy is the real one: remove the microphone duplicate.
      state.entries = state.entries.filter((x) => !(x.who !== 'Them' && Math.abs(x.start - entry.start) < 6000 && similar(x.text, entry.text) >= 0.6));
    } else return;
  }
  let i = state.entries.length;
  while (i > 0 && state.entries[i - 1].start > entry.start) i--;
  state.entries.splice(i, 0, entry);
  renderTranscript();
  detectProject();
  maybePrompt();
}

function updateBehind() {
  const b = state.stt?.behindSec || 0;
  $('behind').textContent = b > 20 ? `transcription ${Math.round(b)} s behind${state.stt?.engine === 'whisper' ? ' (Gemini transcription is faster)' : ''}` : '';
}

function renderTranscript() {
  const box = $('transcript');
  if (!state.entries.length) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty small">Nothing yet.</div>';
    return;
  }
  const atBottom = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight < 40;
  box.innerHTML = state.entries.slice(-400).map((e) => `<div class="t ${e.who === 'Them' ? 'them' : 'me'}${e.unsure ? ' unsure' : ''}"><span class="ts">${hms(new Date(e.start))}</span><span class="who">${esc(e.who === 'Them' ? 'Them' : e.who)}</span><span>${esc(e.text)}${e.unsure ? `<span class="alt" title="The two transcriptions heard different numbers">⚠ or: “${esc(e.alt)}”</span>` : ''}</span></div>`).join('');
  if (atBottom) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

// Which project is being discussed: re-checked at most every 5 s (local, free), and once more when saving.
let projectCheckAt = 0;
let projectRecheck = null;
async function detectProject({ now = false } = {}) {
  if (state.project) return;
  const wait = 5000 - (Date.now() - projectCheckAt);
  if (!now && wait > 0) {
    if (!projectRecheck) projectRecheck = setTimeout(() => {
      projectRecheck = null;
      detectProject();
    }, wait);
    return;
  }
  projectCheckAt = Date.now();
  const text = state.entries.slice(-60).map((e) => e.text).join('\n');
  const found = await detectProjects(text).catch(() => []);
  if (found.length && found.join('|') !== state.autoProjects.join('|')) {
    state.autoProjects = found;
    $('project').options[0].textContent = `Detected: ${found[0].split('/').pop()}`;
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- prompts

async function maybePrompt() {
  const s = state.settings;
  if (state.aiPaused || s.meetingPrompts !== 'auto' || state.prompting || !hasKey(s, pickProvider(s, s.meetingProvider || s.provider))) return;
  const fresh = state.entries.slice(state.lastPromptIdx);
  const d = await shouldPrompt(fresh, {
    sinceLastMs: Date.now() - state.lastPromptAt,
    minGapMs: (s.meetingMinGapSec || 20) * 1000,
    myName: s.myName,
    myLabel: myLabel(),
  });
  if (d.go) runPrompt(d.reason);
}

async function runPrompt(reason, { manual = false } = {}) {
  if (state.prompting) return;
  const s = state.settings || (state.settings = await getSettings());
  if (!hasKey(s, pickProvider(s, s.meetingProvider || s.provider))) {
    toast('Add a Claude or Gemini key in Settings to get prompts. The transcript works without one.', 6000);
    return;
  }
  if (!state.entries.length && manual) {
    toast('Nothing has been said yet.');
    return;
  }
  if (state.aiPaused) {
    toast('AI is stopped. Click ▶ Resume AI to get prompts again.');
    return;
  }
  state.prompting = new AbortController();
  state.lastPromptAt = Date.now();
  state.lastPromptIdx = state.entries.length;
  const card = { id: `p${Date.now()}`, at: Date.now(), thinking: true, reason, manual };
  state.prompts.unshift(card);
  renderPrompts();
  try {
    const res = await makePrompt(state.entries, { projects: projects(), signal: state.prompting.signal, manual });
    Object.assign(card, res, { thinking: false });
    state.cost += (res.cost || 0) + (res.web?.cost || 0);
    if (!res.needed && !res.web?.text) {
      if (manual) Object.assign(card, { empty: true });
      else state.prompts.splice(state.prompts.indexOf(card), 1);
    } else runCheck(card, renderPrompts);
  } catch (err) {
    Object.assign(card, { thinking: false, error: err.message || String(err) });
    state.errors.push(`prompt: ${card.error}`);
  } finally {
    state.prompting = null;
  }
  renderPrompts();
  renderCost();
}

// ---- the second check: shown on each card once it is done (it runs after the card appears) ----

const AI_NAME = { gemini: 'Gemini', anthropic: 'Claude', local: 'your local model' };

function runCheck(obj, render) {
  if (!obj.review || state.aiPaused) {
    delete obj.review;
    return;
  }
  obj.check = { pending: true };
  render();
  checkResult(obj)
    .then((r) => {
      obj.check = r;
      state.cost += r?.cost || 0;
    })
    .catch((err) => {
      obj.check = { error: err.message || String(err) };
    })
    .finally(() => {
      delete obj.review; // the excerpts aren't kept with the meeting
      render();
      renderCost();
    });
}

function checkHtml(c) {
  if (!c) return '';
  if (c.pending) return '<div class="chk pending small">Checking…</div>';
  if (c.error) return `<div class="chk warn small" data-check="unchecked">Not double-checked: ${esc(c.error)}</div>`;
  const local = [];
  if ((c.badQuotes || []).length) local.push(`${c.badQuotes.length === 1 ? 'a quote was' : `${c.badQuotes.length} quotes were`} not found word for word in the file`);
  if ((c.unsupported || []).length) local.push(`numbers not in the files or the transcript: ${c.unsupported.join(', ')}`);
  const findings = [...(c.issues || []).map((i) => `<li><b>“${esc(i.claim)}”</b> — ${esc(i.problem)}${i.evidence ? ` <span class="muted">${esc(i.evidence)}</span>` : ''}${i.fix && i.fix !== 'remove' ? ` <span class="muted">→ ${esc(i.fix)}</span>` : ''}</li>`), ...(c.missed || []).map((m) => `<li>Not covered: ${esc(m)}</li>`), ...local.map((l) => `<li>${esc(l)}</li>`)];
  if (c.verdict === 'ok' && !findings.length) return `<div class="chk ok small" data-check="ok">✓ ${c.by ? (c.self ? `Checked again by ${AI_NAME[c.by]} in a separate review, against the files` : `Checked by ${AI_NAME[c.by]} against the files`) : 'Quotes and numbers found in the files'}</div>`;
  return `<div class="chk warn small" data-check="issues">⚠ ${c.by ? (c.self ? `A second ${AI_NAME[c.by]} review disagrees` : `${AI_NAME[c.by]} disagrees`) : 'Check before using'}:<ul class="plain">${findings.join('')}</ul></div>`;
}

function quoteMark(src) {
  if (src.verified === true) return '<span class="qok" title="Quote found word for word in the file">✓</span>';
  if (src.verified === false) return '<span class="qbad" title="This quote was NOT found word for word in the file — check it">⚠ not in the file</span>';
  return '';
}

// ---- 🔎 Research: the web, about what is being discussed (or what the user typed) ----

async function runResearch(topic = '') {
  state.settings = state.settings || (await getSettings());
  if (state.aiPaused) {
    toast('AI is stopped. Click ▶ Resume AI first.');
    return;
  }
  if (!topic && !state.entries.length) {
    toast('Nothing has been said yet — type a topic in "Ask privately" and click 🔎 Web.');
    return;
  }
  const card = { id: `r${Date.now()}`, kind: 'research', at: Date.now(), thinking: true, topicAsked: topic };
  state.prompts.unshift(card);
  renderPrompts();
  try {
    const res = await researchTopic(state.entries, { projects: projects(), topic });
    Object.assign(card, res, { thinking: false });
    state.cost += res.cost || 0;
  } catch (err) {
    Object.assign(card, { thinking: false, error: err.message || String(err) });
    state.errors.push(`research: ${card.error}`);
  }
  renderPrompts();
  renderCost();
}

// ---- 📝 Minutes: during the meeting (so far) or at the end; Word and text, saved next to the transcript ----

function minutesMarkdown(m) {
  const start = new Date(state.startedAt || Date.now());
  const lines = [`# Minutes — ${m.title || 'Meeting'}`, '', `Date: ${start.toLocaleDateString()} ${pad(start.getHours())}:${pad(start.getMinutes())}`];
  if (projects()[0]) lines.push(`Project: ${projects()[0].split('/').pop()}`);
  if ((m.attendees || []).length) lines.push(`Attendees: ${m.attendees.join(', ')}`);
  if (m.purpose) lines.push(`Purpose: ${m.purpose}`);
  lines.push('', '## Summary', '', m.summary || '', '');
  (m.topics || []).forEach((t, i) => {
    lines.push(`## ${i + 1}. ${t.heading}`, '', t.discussion || '');
    for (const d of t.decisions || []) lines.push(`- **Decision:** ${d}`);
    lines.push('');
  });
  if ((m.decisions || []).length) lines.push('## Decisions', '', ...m.decisions.map((d) => `- ${d}`), '');
  if ((m.action_items || []).length) {
    lines.push('## Action items', '', '| Action | Owner | Due |', '|---|---|---|', ...m.action_items.map((a) => `| ${a.what} | ${a.who || ''} | ${a.when || ''} |`), '');
  }
  if ((m.open_questions || []).length) lines.push('## Open questions', '', ...m.open_questions.map((q) => `- ${q}`), '');
  if (m.next_meeting) lines.push(`Next meeting: ${m.next_meeting}`, '');
  if (m.check?.issues?.length) lines.push('## Points to check', '', ...m.check.issues.map((x) => `- “${x.claim}” — ${x.problem}`), '');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

function minutesWord(m) {
  const start = new Date(state.startedAt || Date.now());
  const blocks = [{ title: `Minutes — ${m.title || 'Meeting'}` }, { label: 'Date', text: `${start.toLocaleDateString()} ${pad(start.getHours())}:${pad(start.getMinutes())}` }];
  if (projects()[0]) blocks.push({ label: 'Project', text: projects()[0].split('/').pop() });
  if ((m.attendees || []).length) blocks.push({ label: 'Attendees', text: m.attendees.join(', ') });
  if (m.purpose) blocks.push({ label: 'Purpose', text: m.purpose });
  blocks.push({ h1: 'Summary' }, { p: m.summary || '' });
  (m.topics || []).forEach((t, i) => {
    blocks.push({ h2: `${i + 1}. ${t.heading}` }, { p: t.discussion || '' });
    for (const d of t.decisions || []) blocks.push({ label: 'Decision', text: d });
  });
  if ((m.decisions || []).length) blocks.push({ h1: 'Decisions' }, ...m.decisions.map((d) => ({ bullet: d })));
  if ((m.action_items || []).length) blocks.push({ h1: 'Action items' }, { table: { head: ['Action', 'Owner', 'Due'], rows: m.action_items.map((a) => [a.what, a.who || '', a.when || '']) } });
  if ((m.open_questions || []).length) blocks.push({ h1: 'Open questions' }, ...m.open_questions.map((q) => ({ bullet: q })));
  if (m.next_meeting) blocks.push({ label: 'Next meeting', text: m.next_meeting });
  if (m.check?.issues?.length) blocks.push({ h1: 'Points to check' }, ...m.check.issues.map((x) => ({ bullet: `“${x.claim}” — ${x.problem}` })));
  blocks.push({ note: 'Written from the meeting transcript by Project Assistant; check before circulating.' });
  return docx(blocks);
}

function downloadMinutes(m, kind) {
  const blob = kind === 'docx' ? minutesWord(m) : new Blob([minutesMarkdown(m)], { type: 'text/markdown' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = `${safeFileName(`Minutes ${m.title || 'meeting'}`)}.${kind}`;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 60000);
}

/** Minutes so far (button), or the final minutes at Stop and save. Saved to the NAS next to the transcript. */
async function makeMinutes(opts = {}) {
  // One run at a time (clicking twice, or Stop and save while minutes are being written, waits for it).
  if (state.minutesRun) {
    await state.minutesRun.catch(() => {});
    if (!opts.final) return state.minutes;
  }
  $('btnMinutes').disabled = true;
  state.minutesRun = writeMinutes(opts);
  try {
    return await state.minutesRun;
  } finally {
    state.minutesRun = null;
    $('btnMinutes').disabled = false;
  }
}

async function writeMinutes({ final = false } = {}) {
  let m = final ? state.summary : null;
  if (!final && state.minutesFinal) {
    // The meeting has ended and its final minutes are saved: no new call for the same transcript.
    toast('The final minutes are saved next to the transcript.');
    renderPrompts();
    return state.minutes;
  }
  if (!m) {
    if (state.aiPaused) {
      toast('AI is stopped. Click ▶ Resume AI first.');
      return null;
    }
    if (state.entries.length < 2) {
      toast('Not enough has been said yet for minutes.');
      return null;
    }
    toast('Writing the minutes…');
    try {
      m = await summarize(state.entries, { projects: projects(), remember: false });
    } catch (err) {
      toast(`Minutes failed: ${err.message}`, 6000);
      return null;
    }
    if (!m) {
      toast('Not enough has been said yet for minutes (or no AI key).');
      return null;
    }
    state.cost += m.cost || 0;
    renderCost();
    if (state.minutesFinal) return state.minutes; // the meeting ended meanwhile: the final minutes stay
  }
  if (final) state.minutesFinal = true;
  const card = state.prompts.find((p) => p.kind === 'minutes') || { id: 'minutes', kind: 'minutes', at: Date.now(), pinned: true };
  Object.assign(card, { minutes: m, final, at: Date.now() });
  if (!state.prompts.includes(card)) state.prompts.unshift(card);
  state.minutes = m;
  try {
    const client = await nasClient();
    const base = nasPathFor().replace(/\.md$/, '');
    await writeTextFile(client, `${base} — Minutes.md`, minutesMarkdown(m));
    await ensureDir(client, base.slice(0, base.lastIndexOf('/')) || '/');
    await client.upload(`${base} — Minutes.docx`, minutesWord(m));
    // Minutes saved earlier under another name (before the title or the project was known) are replaced.
    if (state.minutesBase && state.minutesBase !== base) {
      for (const ext of ['md', 'docx']) await client.remove(`${state.minutesBase} — Minutes.${ext}`, { ignoreMissing: true }).catch(() => {});
    }
    state.minutesBase = base;
    card.savedTo = `${base} — Minutes.docx`;
  } catch (err) {
    state.errors.push(`minutes: ${err.message}`);
    card.savedTo = '';
    toast(`Minutes not saved to the NAS (${err.message}) — use ⬇ Word to keep them.`, 6000);
  }
  renderPrompts();
  return m;
}

function sourceHtml(src) {
  const name = (src.path || '').split('/').pop() || src.ref;
  const page = Number(src.page) || 0; // from the AI's answer: a number only
  const where = page ? ` · ${esc(pageLabel(src.path, page))}` : '';
  // An email inside a .pst mailbox can't be opened on its own; the quote is the useful part.
  if (/\.pst$/i.test(src.path || '')) return `<div class="src">✉ <span title="${esc(src.path)}">${esc(name)}${where}</span> <span class="quote">“${esc(src.quote)}”</span> ${quoteMark(src)}</div>`;
  return `<div class="src">📄 <a data-open="${esc(src.path)}" data-page="${page}" title="${esc(src.path)}">${esc(name)}${where}</a> <span class="quote">“${esc(src.quote)}”</span> ${quoteMark(src)}</div>`;
}

function linksHtml(list) {
  return (list || []).filter((s) => /^https?:\/\//i.test(s.url || '')).map((s) => `<div class="src"><a href="${esc(s.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(s.title || s.url)}</a></div>`).join('');
}

function webHtml(web) {
  if (!web) return '';
  if (web.error) return `<div class="web muted">Online look-up failed: ${esc(web.error)}</div>`;
  // Looked up by both AIs, each with its own search engine: do they give the same numbers?
  let both = '';
  if (web.second) {
    const a = AI_NAME[web.by];
    const b = AI_NAME[web.second.by];
    const self = web.second.self || web.second.by === web.by;
    const verdict = web.agree === true ? `<div class="chk ok small" data-agree="yes">✓ ${self ? `${a} looked it up twice, separately, and found the same values.` : `${a} and ${b} looked it up separately and found the same values.`}</div>`
      : web.agree === false ? `<div class="chk warn small" data-agree="no">⚠ ${self ? `Two separate ${a} look-ups found different values` : `${a} and ${b} found different values`} — check the documents below before relying on it.</div>`
        : `<div class="chk small muted" data-agree="unknown">${self ? `${a} looked it up a second time` : `${b} looked it up too`} (no numbers to compare).</div>`;
    both = `${verdict}<details class="small"${web.agree === false ? ' open' : ''}><summary>${self ? 'What the second look-up found' : `What ${b} found`}</summary><div>${esc(web.second.text)}</div>${linksHtml(web.second.sources)}</details>`;
  }
  return `<div class="web">🌐 ${esc(web.text)}${linksHtml(web.sources)}${both}</div>`;
}

function researchCardHtml(c, i) {
  if (c.thinking) return `<div class="card research" data-i="${i}"><div class="thinking">Searching the web${c.topicAsked ? ` for “${esc(c.topicAsked)}”` : ' about what is being discussed'}…</div></div>`;
  if (c.error) return `<div class="card research" data-i="${i}"><div class="err">Research failed: ${esc(c.error)}</div></div>`;
  const check = c.check ? (c.check.error ? `<div class="chk warn small">Not double-checked: ${esc(c.check.error)}</div>`
    : c.check.verdict === 'ok' ? `<div class="chk ok small" data-check="ok">✓ ${c.check.self ? `A second ${AI_NAME[c.check.by]} search` : AI_NAME[c.check.by]} checked these points on the web.</div>`
      : c.check.verdict === 'issues' ? `<div class="chk warn small" data-check="issues">⚠ ${c.check.self ? `A second ${AI_NAME[c.check.by]} search` : AI_NAME[c.check.by]} disagrees:<ul class="plain">${c.check.issues.map((x) => `<li><b>${esc(x.claim)}</b> — ${esc(x.problem)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>`
        : `<div class="chk small muted" data-check="unclear">The second search couldn't confirm these points — check the links before relying on them.</div>`) : '';
  return `<div class="card research ${c.pinned ? 'pinned' : ''}" data-i="${i}">
    <div class="small muted">🔎 Research${c.topic ? `: ${esc(c.topic)}` : ''}</div>
    <ul class="points">${(c.points || []).map((p) => `<li>${esc(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
    ${c.ask ? `<div class="say">Ask: ${esc(c.ask)}</div>` : ''}${linksHtml(c.sources)}${check}
    <div class="meta"><span>${hms(new Date(c.at))}</span><span class="grow"></span><button data-act="pin" title="Keep at the top">${c.pinned ? 'Unpin' : '📌'}</button></div></div>`;
}

function minutesCardHtml(c, i) {
  const m = c.minutes;
  return `<div class="card minutes pinned" data-i="${i}">
    <div class="small muted">📝 Minutes${c.final ? '' : ' so far'}${m.title ? ` — ${esc(m.title)}` : ''}</div>
    <div>${esc(m.summary || '')}</div>
    <div class="small muted">${(m.topics || []).length} topic(s) · ${(m.decisions || []).length} decision(s) · ${(m.action_items || []).length} action item(s)${c.savedTo ? ` · saved to ${esc(c.savedTo)}` : ''}</div>
    ${checkHtml(m.check ? (m.check.error ? { error: m.check.error } : m.check) : null)}
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:4px"><button data-act="minutes-docx">⬇ Word</button><button data-act="minutes-md">⬇ Text</button></div></div>`;
}

function promptCardHtml(c, i) {
  if (c.kind === 'research') return researchCardHtml(c, i);
  if (c.kind === 'minutes') return minutesCardHtml(c, i);
  if (c.thinking) return `<div class="card" data-i="${i}"><div class="thinking">Thinking${c.manual ? '' : ` (${esc(c.reason)})`}…</div></div>`;
  if (c.error) return `<div class="card" data-i="${i}"><div class="err">${esc(c.error)}</div></div>`;
  if (c.empty) return `<div class="card" data-i="${i}"><div class="muted small">Nothing useful to add right now${c.topic ? ` about ${esc(c.topic)}` : ''}.</div></div>`;
  const fresh = Date.now() - c.at < 15000;
  const sug = (c.suggestions || []).map((s) => {
    const general = !(s.sources || []).length && /^general knowledge/i.test(s.why || '');
    return `<div class="say">${esc(s.say)}</div><div class="why">${general ? '<span class="tag" title="Not from your files — check it">general knowledge</span> ' : ''}${esc(general ? String(s.why || '').replace(/^general knowledge:\s*/i, '') : s.why)}</div>${(s.sources || []).map(sourceHtml).join('')}`;
  }).join('<hr class="sep">');
  return `<div class="card ${fresh ? 'new' : ''} ${c.pinned ? 'pinned' : ''}" data-i="${i}">
    ${c.topic ? `<div class="small muted">${esc(c.topic)}</div>` : ''}${sug}${checkHtml(c.check)}${webHtml(c.web)}
    <div class="meta"><span>${hms(new Date(c.at))}</span><span class="grow"></span>
      ${c.id === 'summary' ? '' : `<button data-act="good" title="Useful — prefer these documents for this topic">👍</button>
      <button data-act="bad" title="Not useful — avoid these documents for this topic">👎</button>`}
      ${c.id === 'summary' || !c.topic ? '' : '<button data-act="research" title="Search the web about this topic">🔎</button>'}
      <button data-act="speak" title="Read aloud (use a headset)">🔊</button>
      <button data-act="pin" title="Keep at the top">${c.pinned ? 'Unpin' : '📌'}</button></div></div>`;
}

function renderPrompts() {
  const list = [...state.prompts].sort((a, b) => (b.pinned ? 1 : 0) - (a.pinned ? 1 : 0));
  const html = list.length ? list.slice(0, 30).map((c) => promptCardHtml(c, state.prompts.indexOf(c))).join('') : '<div class="empty small">Prompts appear here when a question or a new topic comes up.</div>';
  $('prompts').innerHTML = html;
  const pipList = state.pip?.document.getElementById('pipList');
  if (pipList) pipList.innerHTML = html + answersHtml();
}

async function onPromptClick(e) {
  const open = e.target.closest('[data-open]');
  if (open) {
    openFile(open.dataset.open, Number(open.dataset.page) || 0);
    return;
  }
  const sp = e.target.closest('[data-act="speak"], [data-act="speak-answer"]');
  if (sp) {
    let text = '';
    if (sp.dataset.act === 'speak-answer') {
      const a = state.answers[Number(sp.closest('[data-answer]')?.dataset.answer)];
      text = a ? [a.answer, a.say ? `You could say: ${a.say}` : ''].filter(Boolean).join('. ') : '';
    } else {
      const c = state.prompts[Number(sp.closest('[data-i]')?.dataset.i)];
      text = c ? (c.suggestions || []).map((s) => s.say).join('. ') : '';
    }
    if (sp.dataset.speaking) {
      stopSpeaking();
      return;
    }
    sp.dataset.speaking = '1';
    try {
      const r = await speak(text, state.settings || (await getSettings()));
      if (r.fallback) toast(`The Gemini voice couldn't be used (${r.fallback}); this computer's voice was used.`);
    } finally {
      delete sp.dataset.speaking;
    }
    return;
  }
  const b = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
  const cardEl = e.target.closest('[data-i]');
  if (!b || !cardEl) return;
  const c = state.prompts[Number(cardEl.dataset.i)];
  if (!c) return;
  if (b.dataset.act === 'pin') c.pinned = !c.pinned;
  if (b.dataset.act === 'research') {
    runResearch(c.topic);
    return;
  }
  if (b.dataset.act === 'minutes-docx' || b.dataset.act === 'minutes-md') {
    downloadMinutes(c.minutes, b.dataset.act === 'minutes-docx' ? 'docx' : 'md');
    return;
  }
  if (b.dataset.act === 'good' || b.dataset.act === 'bad') {
    const paths = [...new Set((c.suggestions || []).flatMap((s) => (s.sources || []).map((x) => x.path)).filter(Boolean))];
    const keywords = [c.topic, ...(c.suggestions || []).map((s) => s.say)].join(' ');
    for (const p of paths) await addHint(keywords, p, b.dataset.act === 'good' ? 'prefer' : 'avoid').catch(() => {});
    toast(paths.length ? (b.dataset.act === 'good' ? 'Learned: these documents are right for this topic.' : 'Learned: skip these documents for this topic.') : 'Noted.');
  }
  renderPrompts();
}

async function openFile(path, page) {
  if (!path) return;
  try {
    const file = await (await nasClient()).getBlob('/' + path);
    if (!file) throw new Error('not on the NAS yet (mirror the folder first)');
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file) + (/\.pdf$/i.test(path) && page ? `#page=${page}` : '');
    window.open(url, '_blank');
  } catch (err) {
    toast(`Couldn't open ${path.split('/').pop()}: ${err.message}`, 6000);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- private questions

function answersHtml() {
  return state.answers.map((a, n) => (a.thinking ? `<div class="card private"><div class="small"><b>You asked:</b> ${esc(a.q)}</div><div class="thinking">Looking…</div></div>` : `<div class="card private" data-answer="${n}">
      <div class="small row nowrap"><span class="grow"><b>You asked:</b> ${esc(a.q)}</span>${a.error ? '' : '<button data-act="speak-answer" title="Read aloud (use a headset)">🔊</button>'}</div>
      ${a.error ? `<div class="err">${esc(a.error)}</div>` : `<div>${esc(a.answer)}</div>${a.say ? `<div class="say" style="margin-top:4px">Say: ${esc(a.say)}</div>` : ''}${(a.sources || []).map(sourceHtml).join('')}${checkHtml(a.check)}${webHtml(a.web)}`}
    </div>`)).join('');
}

function renderAnswers() {
  $('answers').innerHTML = answersHtml();
  renderPrompts();
}

async function ask(q) {
  q = String(q || '').trim();
  if (!q) return;
  state.settings = state.settings || (await getSettings());
  if (state.aiPaused) {
    toast('AI is stopped. Click ▶ Resume AI to ask questions again.');
    return;
  }
  const a = { q, at: Date.now(), thinking: true };
  state.answers.unshift(a);
  renderAnswers();
  try {
    const res = await answerQuestion(q, state.entries, { projects: projects() });
    Object.assign(a, res, { thinking: false });
    state.cost += (res.cost || 0) + (res.web?.cost || 0);
    runCheck(a, renderAnswers);
  } catch (err) {
    Object.assign(a, { thinking: false, error: err.message || String(err) });
  }
  renderAnswers();
  renderCost();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- saving

function markdown() {
  const start = new Date(state.startedAt || Date.now());
  const date = `${start.getFullYear()}-${pad(start.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(start.getDate())}`;
  const mins = Math.round(((state.entries.at(-1)?.end || Date.now()) - state.startedAt) / 60000);
  const proj = projects()[0];
  const lines = [`# ${state.summary?.title || 'Teams meeting'} — ${date} ${pad(start.getHours())}:${pad(start.getMinutes())}`, ''];
  if (proj) lines.push(`Project: ${proj}`);
  const how = { gemini: 'with Gemini', both: 'with Gemini, checked by Whisper' }[state.sttEngine || state.stt?.engine] || 'on this computer (Whisper)';
  lines.push(`Length: about ${Math.max(1, mins)} min · Transcribed ${how}${state.cost ? ` · AI cost ${money(state.cost)}` : ''}`, '');
  if (state.recordedTo) lines.push(`Audio recording (on the computer): ${state.recordedTo}`, '');
  const sm = state.summary;
  if (sm) {
    lines.push('## Summary', '', sm.summary || '', '');
    if (sm.decisions?.length) lines.push('## Decisions', '', ...sm.decisions.map((d) => `- ${d}`), '');
    if (sm.action_items?.length) lines.push('## Action items', '', ...sm.action_items.map((a) => `- [ ] ${a.what}${a.who ? ` — ${a.who}` : ''}${a.when ? ` (${a.when})` : ''}`), '');
    if (sm.open_questions?.length) lines.push('## Open questions', '', ...sm.open_questions.map((q) => `- ${q}`), '');
    const ck = sm.check;
    if (ck?.by) {
      const found = [...(ck.issues || []).map((i) => `- “${i.claim}” — ${i.problem}${i.fix && i.fix !== 'remove' ? ` (→ ${i.fix})` : ''}`), ...(ck.missed || []).map((m) => `- Not covered: ${m}`)];
      lines.push(ck.self ? `## Checked again by ${AI_NAME[ck.by]} (separate review)` : `## Checked by ${AI_NAME[ck.by]}`, '', ...(found.length ? found : ['No problems found against the transcript.']), '');
    }
  }
  const shown = state.prompts.filter((p) => !p.thinking && !p.error && !p.empty);
  if (shown.length) {
    lines.push('## Prompts shown', '');
    for (const p of [...shown].reverse()) {
      const flag = p.check?.verdict === 'issues' ? ' ⚠ (the second check disagreed)' : '';
      for (const sgt of p.suggestions || []) lines.push(`- ${hms(new Date(p.at))} — ${sgt.say}${(sgt.sources || []).length ? ` _(${sgt.sources.map((x) => `${x.path.split('/').pop()}${x.page ? ` ${pageLabel(x.path, x.page)}` : ''}${x.verified === false ? ' — quote not found in the file' : ''}`).join('; ')})_` : ''}${flag}`);
      if (p.web?.text) lines.push(`- ${hms(new Date(p.at))} — 🌐 ${p.web.text.replace(/\n+/g, ' ')}`);
    }
    lines.push('');
  }
  const researched = state.prompts.filter((p) => p.kind === 'research' && !p.thinking && !p.error);
  if (researched.length) {
    lines.push('## Research', '');
    for (const r of [...researched].reverse()) {
      lines.push(`### ${hms(new Date(r.at))} — ${r.topic || r.topicAsked || 'Research'}`, '', ...(r.points || []).map((x) => `- ${x}`));
      if (r.ask) lines.push(`- Ask: ${r.ask}`);
      for (const src of (r.sources || []).filter((x) => /^https?:/.test(x.url || ''))) lines.push(`- [${src.title || src.url}](${src.url})`);
      lines.push('');
    }
  }
  const asked = state.answers.filter((a) => !a.thinking);
  if (asked.length) {
    lines.push('## Private questions', '');
    for (const a of [...asked].reverse()) lines.push(`- **${a.q}** — ${a.error ? `(failed: ${a.error})` : a.answer}`);
    lines.push('');
  }
  lines.push('## Transcript', '');
  for (const e of state.entries) lines.push(`**[${hms(new Date(e.start))}] ${e.who === 'Them' ? 'Them' : e.who}:** ${e.text}${e.unsure ? ` _(unsure — the other transcription heard: “${e.alt}”)_` : ''}  `);
  return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
}

// Never inside a mirrored project folder: the next one-way sync would delete a file that isn't on the computer.
function meetingRoot() {
  const want = `/${String(state.settings.meetingFolder || '/Meetings').replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')}`;
  const inside = state.mirrorPaths.some((p) => want === p || want.startsWith(`${p}/`) || p === '/');
  return inside || want === '/' ? '/Meetings' : want;
}

function nasPathFor() {
  const start = new Date(state.startedAt);
  const stamp = `${start.getFullYear()}-${pad(start.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(start.getDate())} ${pad(start.getHours())}${pad(start.getMinutes())}`;
  const proj = projects()[0];
  const folder = `${meetingRoot()}${proj ? `/${safeFileName(proj.split('/').pop())}` : ''}`;
  return `${folder}/${stamp} ${safeFileName(state.summary?.title || 'Teams meeting')}.md`;
}

async function save(final = false) {
  if (!state.startedAt || !state.entries.length) return;
  await detectProject({ now: true });
  const rec = {
    id: state.meetingId,
    title: state.summary?.title || 'Teams meeting',
    startedAt: state.startedAt,
    endedAt: final ? Date.now() : null,
    project: projects()[0] || '',
    segments: state.entries,
    prompts: state.prompts.filter((p) => !p.thinking).map(({ hitPaths, review, ...p }) => p),
    answers: state.answers.filter((a) => !a.thinking).map(({ review, ...a }) => a),
    summary: state.summary,
    cost: state.cost,
    errors: state.errors.slice(-20),
    savedPath: state.savedPath,
  };
  await put('meetings', rec).catch(() => {});
  try {
    const client = await nasClient();
    const path = nasPathFor();
    await writeTextFile(client, path, markdown());
    if (state.savedPath && state.savedPath !== path) await client.remove(state.savedPath, { ignoreMissing: true }).catch(() => {});
    if (state.indexedPath && state.indexedPath !== path) await unindexMeeting(state.indexedPath);
    state.savedPath = path;
    state.savedAt = Date.now();
    rec.savedPath = path;
    await put('meetings', rec).catch(() => {});
    if (final) await indexMeeting(path);
    $('saved').textContent = `Saved ${hms(new Date())} → ${path}`;
  } catch (err) {
    $('saved').textContent = `Not saved to the NAS (${err.message}) — kept on this computer.`;
    state.errors.push(`save: ${err.message}`);
  }
}

// Past meetings are searchable like any project file ("what did we agree on the deck width last week?").
async function indexMeeting(nasPath) {
  try {
    const md = markdown();
    const path = nasPath.replace(/^\/+/, '');
    const w = new IndexWriter();
    await w.addFile(
      { path, rel: path, folderId: 'meeting', size: md.length, mtime: Date.now(), ext: 'md', project: projects()[0] || '', method: 'meeting', chars: md.length, note: '', indexedAt: Date.now() },
      chunkText(md),
    );
    await w.finish();
    state.indexedPath = nasPath;
  } catch (err) {
    state.errors.push(`index: ${err.message}`);
  }
}

async function unindexMeeting(nasPath) {
  try {
    const w = new IndexWriter();
    await w.removeFile(nasPath.replace(/^\/+/, ''));
    await w.finish();
  } catch {
    /* not indexed */
  }
}

let cancelAutosave = null;
function scheduleAutosave() {
  cancelAutosave?.();
  cancelAutosave = after(60000, async () => {
    if (!state.listening) return;
    await save(false);
    scheduleAutosave();
  });
}

function renderSummary() {
  const sm = state.summary;
  if (!sm) return;
  const card = { id: 'summary', at: Date.now(), topic: 'Meeting summary', suggestions: [{ say: sm.summary, why: [sm.decisions?.length ? `${sm.decisions.length} decision(s)` : '', sm.action_items?.length ? `${sm.action_items.length} action item(s)` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · '), sources: [] }], pinned: true, check: sm.check ? (sm.check.error ? { error: sm.check.error } : { ...sm.check, verdict: sm.check.verdict }) : null };
  state.prompts.unshift(card);
  renderPrompts();
}

function renderCost() {
  $('cost').textContent = state.cost ? `AI ${money(state.cost)}` : '';
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- meters and clock

function updateMeter(id, ch, offText) {
  const el = $(id);
  const fill = el.querySelector('.fill');
  const peak = el.querySelector('.peak');
  const st = el.querySelector('.state');
  if (!ch) {
    el.className = 'meter off';
    fill.style.width = '0';
    peak.style.left = '0';
    st.textContent = offText;
    return;
  }
  ch.level *= 0.9; // decays between frames, so the bar falls when sound stops
  fill.style.width = `${Math.round(Math.min(1, ch.level) * 100)}%`;
  peak.style.left = `${Math.round(Math.min(1, ch.peak) * 100)}%`;
  const quiet = ch.silentFor(15000);
  const hearing = Date.now() - ch.lastSoundAt < 1500;
  el.className = `meter ${quiet ? 'quiet' : hearing ? 'live' : ''}`;
  st.textContent = quiet ? 'no sound' : hearing ? 'hearing sound' : 'quiet';
  return quiet;
}

setInterval(() => {
  const themQuiet = updateMeter('mThem', state.them, 'off');
  updateMeter('mMe', state.me, $('chkMic').checked ? 'off' : 'not used');
  if (state.listening && state.them && themQuiet && $('warn').hidden) {
    warn(state.themKind === 'app'
      ? `No sound from the meeting for 15 s. Chrome hears the Teams app only through what Windows plays on its <b>default speakers</b>:
        <ul class="tight"><li>Share again with <b>Entire screen</b> (not a window) and tick <b>Also share system audio</b>.</li>
        <li>Hearing Teams in a <b>headset</b>? In Teams → Settings → Devices, set <b>Speaker</b> to the same device as Windows' sound output (Windows Settings → System → Sound → Output) — or make the headset the Windows output. Teams can play to a different device from everything else, and Chrome can't hear that.</li>
        <li>Or open the meeting in Teams in Chrome (teams.microsoft.com) and click <b>Teams in Chrome</b> here — that always works.</li></ul>`
      : 'No sound from the meeting for 15 s. If people are talking: share again, pick the Teams tab, and keep <b>Also share tab audio</b> on.');
  } else if (state.them && !themQuiet && /No sound from the meeting/.test($('warn').textContent)) warn('');
  if (state.recording) renderRecording();
  if (state.listening) {
    const s = Math.round((Date.now() - state.startedAt) / 1000);
    $('clock').textContent = `● ${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${pad(s % 60)}`;
  }
  const pipDoc = state.pip?.document;
  if (pipDoc) {
    for (const [id, ch] of [['pThem', state.them], ['pMe', state.me]]) {
      const f = pipDoc.getElementById(id);
      if (f) f.style.width = `${Math.round(Math.min(1, ch?.level || 0) * 100)}%`;
    }
  }
}, 100);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- always-on-top window

async function popOut() {
  if (state.pip) {
    state.pip.focus();
    return;
  }
  if (!('documentPictureInPicture' in window)) {
    toast('This Chrome version cannot keep a window on top. Keep this window beside Teams instead.', 6000);
    return;
  }
  try {
    const pip = await window.documentPictureInPicture.requestWindow({ width: 380, height: 520 });
    for (const link of document.querySelectorAll('link[rel="stylesheet"]')) {
      const l = pip.document.createElement('link');
      l.rel = 'stylesheet';
      l.href = link.href;
      pip.document.head.append(l);
    }
    pip.document.title = 'Prompts';
    pip.document.body.className = 'pip';
    pip.document.body.innerHTML = `<div class="pip-wrap">
      <div class="pip-meters"><span>Meeting</span><div class="bar meter"><div class="fill" id="pThem"></div></div><span>Me</span><div class="bar meter"><div class="fill" id="pMe"></div></div><button id="pipAskNow">Ask now</button><button id="pipResearch" title="Search the web about this">🔎</button><button id="pipAi" title="Stop / resume AI">⏹ Stop AI</button></div>
      <div class="pip-list" id="pipList"></div>
      <form id="pipAsk" class="row nowrap"><input id="pipQ" class="grow" type="text" placeholder="Ask privately…" autocomplete="off"><button class="primary" type="submit">Ask</button></form>
    </div>`;
    pip.document.getElementById('pipAsk').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const q = pip.document.getElementById('pipQ');
      ask(q.value);
      q.value = '';
    });
    pip.document.getElementById('pipAskNow').addEventListener('click', () => runPrompt('asked', { manual: true }));
    pip.document.getElementById('pipAi').addEventListener('click', toggleAi);
    pip.document.getElementById('pipResearch').addEventListener('click', () => runResearch());
    renderAi();
    pip.document.getElementById('pipList').addEventListener('click', onPromptClick);
    pip.addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
      state.pip = null;
    });
    state.pip = pip;
    renderPrompts();
  } catch (err) {
    toast(`Couldn't pop out: ${err.message}`, 6000);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- settings and wiring

async function loadProjects() {
  const list = (await kvGet('projects')) || [];
  const sel = $('project');
  for (const p of list) {
    const o = document.createElement('option');
    o.value = p;
    o.textContent = p.split('/').pop();
    sel.append(o);
  }
}

function fillSettings() {
  for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('#setDlg [data-key]')) {
    const v = state.settings[el.dataset.key];
    if (el.type === 'checkbox') el.checked = !!v;
    else el.value = v ?? '';
  }
}

// The model for the meeting's AI (the same setting as in Settings), picked from the models the key can use.
const MODEL_KEY = { anthropic: 'anthropicModel', gemini: 'geminiModel' };
async function renderMeetingModel() {
  const s = state.settings;
  const provider = pickProvider(s, s.meetingProvider || s.provider);
  $('mtgModelLabel').textContent = `${provider === 'gemini' ? 'Gemini' : 'Claude'} model (also used for emails and the chat)`;
  let models = [];
  if (hasKey(s, provider)) models = await listModels(s, provider).catch(() => []);
  if (!models.length && provider === 'anthropic') models = ['claude-opus-5-5', 'claude-sonnet-5-5', 'claude-haiku-5-5'];
  fillModelSelect($('mtgModel'), modelChoices(provider, models, { autoModel: provider === 'gemini' ? pickGeminiFlash(models) || '' : '' }), s[MODEL_KEY[provider]]);
  $('mtgModel').dataset.provider = provider;
  $('mtgModelOther').hidden = $('mtgModel').value !== '__other';
}
$('mtgModel').addEventListener('change', async () => {
  const sel = $('mtgModel');
  if (sel.value === '__other') {
    $('mtgModelOther').hidden = false;
    $('mtgModelOther').focus();
    return;
  }
  $('mtgModelOther').hidden = true;
  state.settings = await saveSettings({ [MODEL_KEY[sel.dataset.provider]]: sel.value });
  toast(`Model: ${sel.value}`);
});
$('mtgModelOther').addEventListener('change', async () => {
  const v = $('mtgModelOther').value.trim();
  if (!v) return;
  state.settings = await saveSettings({ [MODEL_KEY[$('mtgModel').dataset.provider]]: v });
  await renderMeetingModel();
});

$('setDlg').addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (!el.dataset.key) return;
  const v = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : el.type === 'number' ? Number(el.value) || 20 : el.value;
  state.settings = await saveSettings({ [el.dataset.key]: v });
  if (el.dataset.key === 'meetingStt' && state.listening) toast('The new transcription choice applies to the next meeting.');
  if (el.dataset.key === 'meetingProvider') renderMeetingModel();
});

$('btnSettings').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  state.settings = await getSettings();
  fillSettings();
  renderMeetingModel();
  const la = await localAI.check();
  $('localAiNote').textContent = la === 'available' ? "Chrome's built-in AI is available on this computer: it decides when a prompt is worth paying for." : "Chrome's built-in AI isn't available on this computer; simple rules decide when to prompt instead.";
  $('setDlg').showModal();
});
$('btnApp').addEventListener('click', () => listen('app'));
$('btnTab').addEventListener('click', () => listen('tab'));
$('chkMic').addEventListener('change', async () => {

  if ($('chkMic').checked) await startMic();
  else stopMic();
});
$('btnStop').addEventListener('click', stopAndSave);

// ---- the kill switch ----
function renderAi() {
  const b = $('btnAi');
  b.textContent = state.aiPaused ? '▶ Resume AI' : '⏹ Stop AI';
  b.className = state.aiPaused ? 'primary' : 'danger';
  b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(state.aiPaused));
  const pip = state.pip?.document.getElementById('pipAi');
  if (pip) pip.textContent = state.aiPaused ? '▶ Resume AI' : '⏹ Stop AI';
}
async function toggleAi() {
  if (state.aiPaused) {
    await resumeAI();
    state.aiPaused = false;
    toast('AI resumed.');
  } else {
    await stopAllAI(); // cancels the calls in progress here and in every other part of the extension
    state.aiPaused = true;
    state.prompting?.abort();
    toast('AI stopped: no prompts, answers or Gemini transcription until you resume. Listening and saving carry on.', 6000);
  }
  renderAi();
}
$('btnAi').addEventListener('click', toggleAi);
chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((m) => {
  if (m?.type === 'ai.stopAll' || m?.type === 'ai.resumed') {
    state.aiPaused = m.type === 'ai.stopAll';
    if (state.aiPaused) state.prompting?.abort();
    renderAi();
  }
  return false;
});
$('btnAsk').addEventListener('click', () => runPrompt('asked', { manual: true }));
$('btnResearch').addEventListener('click', () => runResearch());
$('btnMinutes').addEventListener('click', () => makeMinutes());
$('btnWeb').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const q = $('askInput').value.trim();
  $('askInput').value = '';
  runResearch(q);
});
$('btnPip').addEventListener('click', popOut);
attachDictation($('askMic'), $('askInput'), { getSettings, onError: (m) => toast(m, 6000) });
$('prompts').addEventListener('click', onPromptClick);
$('answers').addEventListener('click', onPromptClick);
$('askForm').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  ask($('askInput').value);
  $('askInput').value = '';
});
$('project').addEventListener('change', () => {
  state.project = $('project').value;
});
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', (e) => {
  if (state.listening) {
    save(false);
    e.preventDefault();
    e.returnValue = '';
  }
});

state.onText = onText; // for the automated tests
state.nasPathFor = nasPathFor;
state.renderPrompts = renderPrompts;
state.settings = await getSettings();
state.aiPaused = !!state.settings.aiPaused;
renderAi();
state.mirrorPaths = (await projectFolders({ withPerm: false }).catch(() => [])).map((f) => f.nasPath);
await loadProjects();
state.ready = true;
