        } catch {
          throw new WebDAVError(describeStatus(0), { status: 0, retryable: true });
        }
        // File Browser answers 403 to a wrong username or password.
        if (res.status === 403 || res.status === 401) throw new WebDAVError(describeStatus(401), { status: 401 });
        if (!res.ok) throw new WebDAVError(describeStatus(res.status, 'POST'), { status: res.status, retryable: res.status >= 500 });
        const token = (await res.text()).trim();
        if (token.split('.').length !== 3) throw new WebDAVError('The NAS answered the sign-in with something unexpected (is this File Browser?)');
        this.token = token;
        return token;
      })().finally(() => {
        this.signingIn = null;
      });
    }
    return this.signingIn;
  }

  /** A signed-in request; signs in again once if the token ran out. */
  async send(method, url, opts = {}) {
    const go = async () => xhrSend(method, url, { ...opts, headers: { ...(opts.headers || {}), 'X-Auth': await this.signIn() } });
    try {
      return await go();
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status !== 401) throw err;
      this.token = null;
      await this.signIn(true);
      return go();
    }
  }

  async list(path, opts = {}) {
    const res = await this.send('GET', this.api('resources', path, { dir: true }), { ...opts, timeout: 300000 });
    let data;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(res.text);
    } catch {
      throw new WebDAVError('The NAS sent a folder listing that could not be read (is this really File Browser?)');
    }
    if (!data.isDir) return [];
    const folder = normalizePath(path);
    return (data.items || []).map((it) => {
      const p = normalizePath(`${folder}/${it.name}`);
      const modified = it.modified ? new Date(it.modified) : null;
      return { path: p, name: it.name, isDir: !!it.isDir, size: it.isDir ? null : Number(it.size) || 0, modified: modified && !Number.isNaN(modified.getTime()) ? modified : null };
    });
  }

  /** Info about a path, or null when it doesn't exist (read from its folder's listing: never the file itself). */
  async stat(path, opts = {}) {
    const p = normalizePath(path);
    if (p === '/') return { path: '/', name: '', isDir: true, size: null, modified: null };
    try {
      const entries = await this.list(parentPath(p), opts);
      return entries.find((e) => e.name === baseName(p)) || null;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 404) return null;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** Create a folder (and any missing parents). Resolves to 'created' or 'exists'. */
  async mkdir(path, opts = {}) {
    const p = normalizePath(path);
    if (p === '/') return 'exists';
    const info = await this.stat(p, opts);
    if (info?.isDir) return 'exists';
    if (info) throw new WebDAVError(`A file named "${baseName(p)}" already exists where a folder is needed`, { status: 409 });
    await this.send('POST', this.api('resources', p, { dir: true }), opts);
    return 'created';
  }

  /** Upload a File/Blob to path (overwrites; missing folders are made). */
  upload(path, file, { onProgress, signal } = {}) {
    return this.send('POST', this.api('resources', path, { query: { override: 'true' } }), {
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' },
      body: file,
      timeout: 0,
      signal,
      onUploadProgress: onProgress,
    });
  }

  async remove(path, { ignoreMissing = false, ...opts } = {}) {
    const p = normalizePath(path);
    if (p === '/') throw new WebDAVError('Refusing to delete the root folder');
    try {
      await this.send('DELETE', this.api('resources', p), opts);
    } catch (err) {
      if (ignoreMissing && err.status === 404) return;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** Server-side rename/move. Never overwrites: fails if the destination exists. */
  async move(from, to, { dir: _dir, ...opts } = {}) {
    const src = normalizePath(from);
    const dst = normalizePath(to);
    if (src === '/' || dst === '/') throw new WebDAVError('Refusing to move the root folder');
    try {
      await this.send('PATCH', this.api('resources', src, { query: { action: 'rename', destination: dst, override: 'false', rename: 'false' } }), opts);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 409) throw new WebDAVError(`"${baseName(dst)}" already exists on the NAS`, { status: 412 });
      throw err;
    }
  }

  async getText(path, opts = {}) {
    try {
      const res = await this.send('GET', this.api('raw', path), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }, ...opts });
      return res.text;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 404) return null;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  async getBlob(path, opts = {}) {
    try {
      const res = await this.send('GET', this.api('raw', path), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }, responseType: 'blob', timeout: 600000, ...opts });
      const lm = Date.parse(res.lastModified || '') || Date.now();
      return new File([res.response], baseName(normalizePath(path)), { lastModified: lm, type: res.response?.type || '' });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 404) return null;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  putText(path, text, { contentType = 'application/json; charset=utf-8', ...opts } = {}) {
    return this.send('POST', this.api('resources', path, { query: { override: 'true' } }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': contentType }, body: text, ...opts });
  }

  /** Where Chrome's download manager can fetch a file from, with the session token it needs. */
  async downloadInfo(path) {
    const token = await this.signIn();
    return { url: this.api('raw', path), headers: [{ name: 'X-Auth', value: token }] };
  }
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/files.js (152 lines) =====
// File tools for the chat, like Desktop Commander's but inside Chrome: list, read and find files in the project
// folders on this computer (read-only: Chrome gave the extension read access) and on the NAS (read and write over
// WebDAV). Changes (write, new folder, move/rename, copy, delete) only happen on the NAS, and wait for the user's
// click unless they switched that off.

import { getAll, getSettings } from './db.js';
import { nasClient, ensureDir } from './nas.js';
import { projectFolders } from './folders.js';
import { permissionState, getFileByPath, getDirByPath } from './fsutil.js';
import { extractFile } from './extract.js';

const MAX_READ = 12000;

/** "nas:/Projects/x" → "/Projects/x"; anything not "local:" is a NAS path. */
export function nasPathOf(p) {
  return `/${String(p || '').replace(/^nas:/i, '').replace(/^\/+/, '')}`.replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
}

async function localFolder(name) {
  const folders = await projectFolders({ withPerm: false }).catch(() => []);
  const m = folders.find((x) => x.name === name || x.handle.name === name) || (!name && folders.length === 1 ? folders[0] : null);
  if (!m) throw new Error(`No project folder called "${name}". Folders: ${folders.map((x) => x.handle.name).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
  if ((await permissionState(m.handle, 'read')) !== 'granted') throw new Error(`No access to "${m.handle.name}" right now: in "NAS files" click Reconnect, or use the NAS copy (nas:${m.nasPath}).`);
  return m;
}

function splitLocal(p) {
  const [folder, ...rest] = String(p).replace(/^local:\/*/i, '').split('/');
  return { folder, rel: rest.filter(Boolean).join('/') };
}

/** Lists a folder: local:<project folder>/<sub> or a NAS path. At most 300 entries. */
export async function listFolder(path = '/') {
  if (/^local:/i.test(path)) {
    const { folder, rel } = splitLocal(path);
    const m = await localFolder(folder);
    const dir = rel ? await getDirByPath(m.handle, rel) : m.handle;
    const out = [];
    for await (const [name, h] of dir.entries()) {
      if (out.length >= 300) break;
      const e = { name, dir: h.kind === 'directory' };
      if (h.kind === 'file') {
        const f = await h.getFile().catch(() => null);
        if (f) Object.assign(e, { size: f.size, modified: new Date(f.lastModified).toISOString().slice(0, 16) });
      }
      out.push(e);
    }
    return { path, entries: out.sort((a, b) => Number(b.dir) - Number(a.dir) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name)) };
  }
  const c = await nasClient();
  const entries = (await c.list(nasPathOf(path))).slice(0, 300).map((e) => ({ name: e.name, dir: !!e.isDir, size: e.isDir ? undefined : e.size, modified: e.modified ? new Date(e.modified).toISOString().slice(0, 16) : undefined }));
  return { path: nasPathOf(path), entries };
}

/** Reads a file's text (PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint and text files), shortened. */
export async function readFile(path, { maxChars = MAX_READ } = {}) {
  let file;
  if (/^local:/i.test(path)) {
    const { folder, rel } = splitLocal(path);
    const m = await localFolder(folder);
    file = await (await getFileByPath(m.handle, rel)).getFile();
  } else {
    const p = nasPathOf(path);
    const blob = await (await nasClient()).getBlob(p);
    if (!blob) throw new Error(`${p} is not on the NAS`);
    file = new File([blob], p.split('/').pop());
  }
  const res = await extractFile(file, { maxChars: maxChars * 2 });
  const text = (res.pages || []).map(([page, t]) => (page ? `[page ${page}]\n${t}` : t)).join('\n\n');
  return { path, size: file.size, chars: text.length, text: text.length > maxChars ? `${text.slice(0, maxChars)}\n[…shortened]` : text };
}

/** Finds files by name in the index (free, instant): every indexed project file, email and meeting. */
export async function findFiles(name, { under = '', limit = 40 } = {}) {
  const words = String(name || '').toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const prefix = String(under || '').replace(/^nas:/i, '').replace(/^\/+/, '').toLowerCase();
  const hits = (await getAll('files'))
    .filter((r) => {
      const p = r.path.toLowerCase();
      return (!prefix || p.startsWith(prefix)) && words.every((w) => p.includes(w));
    })
    .sort((a, b) => (b.mtime || 0) - (a.mtime || 0))
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map((r) => ({ path: r.folderId === 'mail' || r.folderId === 'meeting' ? r.path : `nas:/${r.path.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`, modified: r.mtime ? new Date(r.mtime).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : '', size: r.size }));
  return { hits };
}

/** A plain-words summary of a change, for the confirmation button. */
export function describeChange(op) {
  switch (op.op) {
    case 'write': return `Write ${nasPathOf(op.path)} on the NAS (${String(op.text || '').length} characters)`;
    case 'mkdir': return `Create the folder ${nasPathOf(op.path)} on the NAS`;
    case 'move': return `Move/rename ${nasPathOf(op.path)} → ${nasPathOf(op.to)} on the NAS`;
    case 'copy': return `Copy ${nasPathOf(op.path)} → ${nasPathOf(op.to)} on the NAS`;
    case 'delete': return `Delete ${nasPathOf(op.path)} on the NAS`;
    default: return `Unknown change ${op.op}`;
  }
}

/** Is this NAS path inside a mirrored project folder? (The mirror copies this computer → NAS, one way.) */
async function mirroredFolder(p) {
  const folders = await projectFolders({ withPerm: false }).catch(() => []);
  return folders.find((f) => f.nasPath && (p === f.nasPath || p.startsWith(`${f.nasPath.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/`)));
}

/** Makes a change on the NAS. Returns {done, note?}. */
export async function applyChange(op) {
  const c = await nasClient();
  const p = nasPathOf(op.path);
  if (p === '/' || p.split('/').length < 2) throw new Error('Refused: that is the top of the NAS.');
  const mirrored = await mirroredFolder(p);
  const note = mirrored ? `Note: ${p} is inside the mirrored folder "${mirrored.name}" — the mirror copies this computer to the NAS, so the next sync may undo this change on the NAS.` : undefined;
  switch (op.op) {
    case 'write': {
      await ensureDir(c, p.slice(0, p.lastIndexOf('/')) || '/');
      await c.upload(p, new Blob([String(op.text || '')], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }));
      return { done: true, note };
    }
    case 'mkdir':
      await ensureDir(c, p);
      return { done: true, note };
    case 'move': {
      const to = nasPathOf(op.to);
      const st = await c.stat(p);
      if (!st) throw new Error(`${p} is not on the NAS`);
      await ensureDir(c, to.slice(0, to.lastIndexOf('/')) || '/');
      await c.move(p, to, { dir: !!st.isDir });
      return { done: true, note };
    }
    case 'copy': {
      const to = nasPathOf(op.to);
      const blob = await c.getBlob(p);
      if (!blob) throw new Error(`${p} is not on the NAS (folders can't be copied this way)`);
      await ensureDir(c, to.slice(0, to.lastIndexOf('/')) || '/');
      await c.upload(to, blob);
      return { done: true, note };
    }
    case 'delete': {
      const st = await c.stat(p);
      if (!st) return { done: true, note: 'It was already gone.' };
      await c.remove(p, { dir: !!st.isDir });
      return { done: true, note };
    }
    default:
      throw new Error(`Unknown change ${op.op}`);
  }
}

/** Whether changes wait for the user's click (Settings → Chat; on by default). */
export async function changesNeedClick() {
  return (await getSettings()).chatFileConfirm !== false;
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/folders.js (49 lines) =====
// The project folders: the mirrors saved in "NAS files" (app.html). One fileset — each folder is mirrored to
// the NAS once and indexed once, and email drafts, meeting prompts and the chat all search that index.
// Paths in the index are the files' NAS paths without the leading slash ("Projects/2024-017 Alert/Specs/x.pdf"),
// so a file means the same thing on this computer and on the NAS (where the deep OCR index is built).

import { CONFIG, loadConfig } from './config-store.js';
import { listMirrors } from './store.js';
import { getSettings } from './db.js';
import { permissionState } from './fsutil.js';

/** [{id, name, handle, nasPath, prefix, index, projectMode, lastSync, perm}] for this NAS. */
export async function projectFolders({ withPerm = true } = {}) {
  await loadConfig();
  const settings = await getSettings();
  let mirrors = [];
  try {
    mirrors = await listMirrors();
  } catch {
    mirrors = [];
  }
  mirrors = mirrors.filter((m) => m.serverUrl === CONFIG.serverUrl && m.handle).sort((a, b) => a.created - b.created);
  const out = [];
  for (const m of mirrors) {
    const pref = (settings.folderPrefs || {})[m.id] || {};
    out.push({
      id: m.id,
      name: m.name,
      handle: m.handle,
      nasPath: m.nasPath,
      prefix: String(m.nasPath || '').replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, ''),
      index: pref.index !== false,
      projectMode: pref.projectMode === 'single' ? 'single' : 'subfolders',
      lastSync: m.lastSync || null,
      perm: withPerm ? await permissionState(m.handle, 'read') : undefined,
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * The project a file belongs to. 'subfolders': every folder directly inside the mirrored folder is a project
 * (a drive of project folders). 'single': the mirrored folder itself is one project.
 */
export function projectFor(prefix, rel, mode = 'subfolders') {
  if (mode === 'single') return prefix;
  if (!rel.includes('/')) return ''; // loose files next to the project folders belong to no project
  const first = rel.split('/')[0];
  return prefix ? `${prefix}/${first}` : first;
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/fsutil.js (119 lines) =====
// Helpers around File System Access handles (local folders, the OneDrive bridge folder,
// and OPFS in tests — they all share one interface).

import { kvGet, kvSet } from './db.js';

// The assistant's own files inside a folder (bridge requests, old MailMirror trash); never indexed.
export const META_DIR = '.assistant';
export const META_DIRS = ['.assistant', '.mailmirror'];

export async function getRootHandle(which) {
  return (await kvGet(`handle:${which}`)) || null;
}

export async function setRootHandle(which, handle) {
  await kvSet(`handle:${which}`, handle);
}

// 'granted' | 'prompt' | 'denied' | 'missing'
export async function permissionState(handle, mode = 'readwrite') {
  if (!handle) return 'missing';
  if (typeof handle.queryPermission !== 'function') return 'granted'; // OPFS
  try {
    return await handle.queryPermission({ mode });
  } catch {
    return 'prompt';
  }
}

// Must be called from a user gesture (button click) in a visible page.
export async function requestPermission(handle, mode = 'readwrite') {
  if (!handle) return 'missing';
  if (typeof handle.requestPermission !== 'function') return 'granted';
  return handle.requestPermission({ mode });
}

// Walks a directory tree. Yields { path, handle, file } for files that pass
// `skip(path, isDir)`. Unreadable entries are reported via onError and skipped.
export async function* walk(dir, { skip = () => false, onError = () => {}, prefix = '', withFile = true } = {}) {
  let entries;
  try {
    entries = [];
    for await (const entry of dir.values()) entries.push(entry);
  } catch (err) {
    onError(prefix || '/', err);
    return;
  }
  entries.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const path = prefix ? `${prefix}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;
    if (entry.kind === 'directory') {
      if (skip(path, true)) continue;
      yield* walk(entry, { skip, onError, prefix: path, withFile });
    } else {
      if (skip(path, false)) continue;
      if (!withFile) { yield { path, handle: entry }; continue; }
      try {
        yield { path, handle: entry, file: await entry.getFile() };
      } catch (err) {
        onError(path, err);
      }
    }
  }
}

export async function getDirByPath(root, dirPath, create = false) {
  let dir = root;
  if (!dirPath) return dir;
  for (const seg of dirPath.split('/')) {
    if (!seg) continue;
    dir = await dir.getDirectoryHandle(seg, { create });
  }
  return dir;
}

export function splitPath(path) {
  const i = path.lastIndexOf('/');
  return i < 0 ? ['', path] : [path.slice(0, i), path.slice(i + 1)];
}

export async function getFileByPath(root, path, create = false) {
  const [dirPath, name] = splitPath(path);
  const dir = await getDirByPath(root, dirPath, create);
  return dir.getFileHandle(name, { create });
}

export async function fileExists(root, path) {
  try {
    await getFileByPath(root, path);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

// Streams src (a File/Blob) into root/path, creating folders as needed.
// createWritable writes to a temporary file and swaps it in on close, so a
// failed copy never leaves a half-written destination.
export async function writeFile(root, path, blob) {
  const fh = await getFileByPath(root, path, true);
  const w = await fh.createWritable();
  try {
    await blob.stream().pipeTo(w);
  } catch (err) {
    try { await w.abort(); } catch { /* already closed */ }
    throw err;
  }
  return fh.getFile();
}

export async function removeFile(root, path) {
  const [dirPath, name] = splitPath(path);
  const dir = await getDirByPath(root, dirPath);
  await dir.removeEntry(name);
}

export function stamp(d = new Date()) {
  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}_${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/hp.js (59 lines) =====
// Commands on the HP: the NAS container runs them for the extension (setup-webdav.sh --enable-commands), as its
// limited "nas-runner" user, behind the NAS login and a separate command key (Settings → Run on the HP).
// Nothing runs on this computer: Chrome doesn't allow that.

import { getSettings } from './db.js';
import { nasCreds } from './nas.js';
import { basicAuthHeader } from './util.js';
import { CONFIG, loadConfig } from './config-store.js';

/** The command endpoint: <NAS address>/.run */
async function endpoint() {
  await loadConfig();
  if (!CONFIG.serverUrl || /your-nas\.example/.test(CONFIG.serverUrl)) throw new Error('Connect the NAS first (NAS files → Connect).');
  return new URL('.run', CONFIG.serverUrl.endsWith('/') ? CONFIG.serverUrl : `${CONFIG.serverUrl}/`).href;
}

export async function hpCommandsReady(settings) {
  const s = settings || (await getSettings());
  return Boolean(s.hpCommandKey);
}

/**
 * Runs one command on the HP. Returns {exit, stdout, stderr, ms, timed_out, cwd} or throws with a plain-words
 * reason. `cwd` defaults to the shared folder; `timeout` in seconds (max 600).
 */
export async function runOnHp(cmd, { cwd = '', timeout = 60, signal } = {}) {
  const s = await getSettings();
  if (!s.hpCommandKey) throw new Error('Commands on the HP are not set up: run the NAS setup script with --enable-commands, then paste the command key it prints into Settings → Run on the HP.');
  const c = await nasCreds();
  if (!c) throw new Error('The NAS login is missing (NAS files → Connect).');
  const res = await fetch(await endpoint(), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { authorization: basicAuthHeader(c.username, c.password), 'x-command-key': s.hpCommandKey, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ cmd: String(cmd), cwd: String(cwd || ''), timeout: Math.max(1, Math.min(600, Number(timeout) || 60)) }),
    signal,
    credentials: 'omit',
    cache: 'no-store',
  });
  let data = {};
  try {
    data = await res.json();
  } catch {
    /* not JSON (e.g. a 404 page) */
  }
  if (res.status === 401) throw new Error('The NAS refused the login.');
  if (res.status === 403) throw new Error('Wrong command key (Settings → Run on the HP).');
  if (res.status === 404) throw new Error('Commands are not switched on on the HP: run the NAS setup script with --enable-commands.');
  if (res.status === 409) throw new Error('Another command is still running on the HP.');
  if (!res.ok || data.error) throw new Error(data.error || `The HP answered ${res.status}`);
  // Something other than the command endpoint answered (e.g. File Browser's page): commands aren't set up there.
  if (!('exit' in data)) throw new Error('Commands are not switched on at this NAS address (it has no command endpoint).');
  return data;
}

/** Short text for the chat / UI. */
export function commandResultText(r, max = 6000) {
  const out = [r.stdout ? r.stdout.trimEnd() : '', r.stderr ? `[stderr]\n${r.stderr.trimEnd()}` : ''].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
  return `exit ${r.exit}${r.timed_out ? ' (stopped: time limit)' : ''} · ${r.ms} ms${out ? `\n${out.length > max ? `…${out.slice(-max)}` : out}` : ''}`;
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/index.js (326 lines) =====
// Local full-text index: BM25 ranking over IndexedDB, zero tokens spent.
//
// Layout: `chunks` holds ~1 KB passages, `terms` holds one posting list per
// term as a flat array [chunkId, impact, chunkId, impact, ...]. Impacts are
// BM25 term weights precomputed at index time (length-normalised), so a
// query only reads the posting lists of its own terms.

import { tx, reqP, txDone, kvGet, kvSet, getAll, clear } from './db.js';
import { tokenize, termFreqs } from './text.js';

export const INDEX_VERSION = 1;
const K1 = 1.2;
const B = 0.75;
const DEFAULT_AVGDL = 160;

async function loadState() {
  const stats = (await kvGet('indexStats')) || { n: 0, totalLen: 0 };
  const dead = new Set((await kvGet('deadChunks')) || []);
  return { stats, dead };
}

export class IndexWriter {
  constructor() {
    this.pending = new Map();      // term -> flat [id, impact, ...]
    this.pendingEntries = 0;
    this.ready = loadState().then((s) => {
      Object.assign(this, s);
      this.base = { ...s.stats };
      this.baseDead = new Set(s.dead);
    });
  }

  avgdl() {
    return this.stats.n > 50 ? this.stats.totalLen / this.stats.n : DEFAULT_AVGDL;
  }

  // Replaces whatever the index holds for rec.path.
  // rec: file record (path, size, mtime, ...); chunks: [{text, page}]
  async addFile(rec, chunks) {
    await this.ready;
    await this.removeFile(rec.path);
    const pathTerms = rec.path.replace(/\.[^./]+$/, '').replace(/[/_]+/g, ' ');
    const t = await tx(['chunks', 'files'], 'readwrite');
    const cs = t.objectStore('chunks');
    const prepared = chunks.map((c, ord) => {
      const indexText = ord === 0 ? `${pathTerms}\n${c.text}` : c.text;
      const tf = termFreqs(indexText);
      let len = 0;
      for (const v of tf.values()) len += v;
      return { c, ord, tf, len };
    });
    const idReqs = prepared.map(({ c, ord, len }) =>
      reqP(cs.add({ path: rec.path, ord, page: c.page || 0, text: c.text, len })));
    const ids = await Promise.all(idReqs);
    rec.chunkIds = ids;
    rec.v = INDEX_VERSION;
    t.objectStore('files').put(rec);
    await txDone(t);

    const avgdl = this.avgdl();
    prepared.forEach(({ tf, len }, i) => {
      const id = ids[i];
      this.stats.n += 1;
      this.stats.totalLen += len;
      const norm = K1 * (1 - B + (B * len) / avgdl);
      for (const [term, f] of tf) {
        const impact = Math.max(1, Math.round((100 * f * (K1 + 1)) / (f + norm)));
        let list = this.pending.get(term);
        if (!list) this.pending.set(term, (list = []));
        list.push(id, impact);
        this.pendingEntries += 1;
      }
    });
    if (this.pendingEntries > 150000) await this.flush();
  }

  async removeFile(path) {
    await this.ready;
    const t = await tx(['files', 'chunks'], 'readwrite');
    const fs = t.objectStore('files');
    const old = await reqP(fs.get(path));
    if (old) {
      const cs = t.objectStore('chunks');
      for (const id of old.chunkIds || []) {
        const c = await reqP(cs.get(id));
        if (c) {
          this.stats.n -= 1;
          this.stats.totalLen -= c.len || 0;
          cs.delete(id);
        }
        this.dead.add(id);
      }
      fs.delete(path);
    }
    await txDone(t);
    return !!old;
  }

  async flush() {
    if (!this.pending.size) return;
    const pending = this.pending;
    this.pending = new Map();
    this.pendingEntries = 0;
    const t = await tx(['terms'], 'readwrite');
    const ts = t.objectStore('terms');
    await Promise.all([...pending].map(async ([term, list]) => {
      const cur = await reqP(ts.get(term));
      ts.put({ t: term, p: cur ? cur.p.concat(list) : list });
    }));
    await txDone(t);
  }

  async finish() {
    await this.ready;
    await this.flush();
    // Other writers (the folder index, emails, a meeting window) may have changed the totals meanwhile: this
    // writer's changes are added to the stored values, in one transaction, instead of overwriting them.
    const dn = this.stats.n - this.base.n;
    const dl = this.stats.totalLen - this.base.totalLen;
    const newDead = [...this.dead].filter((id) => !this.baseDead.has(id));
    const t = await tx(['kv'], 'readwrite');
    const st = t.objectStore('kv');
    const cur = (await reqP(st.get('indexStats'))) || { n: 0, totalLen: 0 };
    const stats = { ...cur, n: Math.max(0, (cur.n || 0) + dn), totalLen: Math.max(0, (cur.totalLen || 0) + dl) };
    const dead = new Set((await reqP(st.get('deadChunks'))) || []);
    for (const id of newDead) dead.add(id);
    st.put(stats, 'indexStats');
    st.put([...dead], 'deadChunks');
    await txDone(t);
    if (dead.size > 2000 && dead.size > stats.n * 0.25) {
      await compact(dead);
      const t2 = await tx(['kv'], 'readwrite');
      const st2 = t2.objectStore('kv');
      const now = new Set((await reqP(st2.get('deadChunks'))) || []);
      for (const id of dead) now.delete(id);
      st2.put([...now], 'deadChunks');
      await txDone(t2);
      dead.clear();
      for (const id of now) dead.add(id);
    }
    this.stats = stats;
    this.dead = dead;
    this.base = { ...stats };
    this.baseDead = new Set(dead);
  }
}

// Drops postings that point at deleted chunks.
async function compact(dead) {
  const t = await tx(['terms'], 'readwrite');
  const ts = t.objectStore('terms');
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const req = ts.openCursor();
    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    req.onsuccess = () => {
      const cur = req.result;
      if (!cur) return resolve();
      const p = cur.value.p;
      const kept = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < p.length; i += 2) if (!dead.has(p[i])) kept.push(p[i], p[i + 1]);
      if (!kept.length) cur.delete();
      else if (kept.length !== p.length) cur.update({ t: cur.value.t, p: kept });
      cur.continue();
    };
  });
  await txDone(t);
}

// Document frequency for each term (approximate: includes not-yet-compacted dead ids).
export async function termStats(terms) {
  const t = await tx(['terms']);
  const ts = t.objectStore('terms');
  const out = new Map();
  await Promise.all(terms.map(async (term) => {
    const rec = await reqP(ts.get(term));
    out.set(term, rec ? rec.p.length / 2 : 0);
  }));
  return out;
}

// Picks the terms of a long text that best identify it (tf·idf against the
// index). Terms the index has never seen are useless for retrieval and are
// dropped; so are terms present in a large share of all chunks.
export async function selectQueryTerms(text, maxTerms = 24, boostText = '') {
  const tf = new Map();
  for (const t of tokenize(text)) tf.set(t, (tf.get(t) || 0) + 1);
  for (const t of tokenize(boostText)) tf.set(t, (tf.get(t) || 0) + 3);
  if (!tf.size) return new Map();
  const { stats } = await loadState();
  const N = Math.max(1, stats.n);
  const df = await termStats([...tf.keys()]);
  const scored = [];
  for (const [term, f] of tf) {
    const d = df.get(term) || 0;
    if (!d) continue;
    if (N > 200 && d / N > 0.3) continue;
    const idf = Math.log(1 + (N - d + 0.5) / (d + 0.5));
    scored.push([term, Math.log(1 + f) * idf]);
  }
  scored.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  const top = scored.slice(0, maxTerms);
  const max = top.length ? top[0][1] : 1;
  return new Map(top.map(([t, s]) => [t, 0.5 + s / max]));
}

// query: string or Map(term -> weight).
// Returns [{id, path, page, text, score}] — at most k, at most perFile per
// file, total text within charBudget.
// preferRecent: when the talk is about updates, schedules or forecasts, newer files rank higher
// (a file changed this month scores up to 1.8x; one untouched for years about 1x).
export async function search(query, {
  k = 8, charBudget = 7000, perFile = 2, maxChunkChars = 1200,
  boostPrefixes = [], excludeIds = new Set(), pathPrefix = '',
  onlyPaths = null, excludePaths = null, preferRecent = false, pathFilter = null,
} = {}) {
  const weights = query instanceof Map ? query : new Map([...new Set(tokenize(query))].map((t) => [t, 1]));
  if (!weights.size) return [];
  const { stats, dead } = await loadState();
  const N = Math.max(1, stats.n);
  const t = await tx(['terms', 'chunks']);
  const ts = t.objectStore('terms');
  const lists = await Promise.all([...weights.keys()].map((term) => reqP(ts.get(term))));
  const scores = new Map();
  [...weights.values()].forEach((w, i) => {
    const rec = lists[i];
    if (!rec) return;
    const p = rec.p;
    const df = p.length / 2;
    const idf = Math.log(1 + (N - df + 0.5) / (df + 0.5));
    for (let j = 0; j < p.length; j += 2) {
      const id = p[j];
      if (dead.has(id) || excludeIds.has(id)) continue;
      scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) || 0) + w * idf * (p[j + 1] / 100));
    }
  });
  // A path filter can discard most top hits, so look deeper when one is set.
  const depth = onlyPaths || pathPrefix || pathFilter ? 2000 : Math.max(60, k * 8);
  const cands = [...scores].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, depth);
  const cs = t.objectStore('chunks');
  const recs = await Promise.all(cands.map(([id]) => reqP(cs.get(id))));
  const boosts = boostPrefixes.filter(Boolean).map((p) => p.toLowerCase());
  const mtimes = new Map();
  if (preferRecent) {
    const ft = await tx(['files']);
    const fs = ft.objectStore('files');
    const paths = [...new Set(recs.filter(Boolean).map((c) => c.path))];
    await Promise.all(paths.map(async (p) => mtimes.set(p, (await reqP(fs.get(p)))?.mtime || 0)));
  }
  const now = Date.now();
  const hits = [];
  recs.forEach((c, i) => {
    if (!c) return;
    if (pathPrefix && !c.path.startsWith(pathPrefix)) return;
    if (onlyPaths && !onlyPaths.has(c.path)) return;
    if (excludePaths && excludePaths.has(c.path)) return;
    if (pathFilter && !pathFilter(c.path)) return;
    let score = cands[i][1];
    const lp = c.path.toLowerCase();
    if (boosts.some((b) => lp.startsWith(b + '/') || lp === b)) score *= 1.6;
    if (preferRecent) {
      const ageDays = Math.max(0, (now - (mtimes.get(c.path) || 0)) / 86400000);
      score *= 1 + 0.8 * Math.exp(-ageDays / 120);
    }
    hits.push({ id: c.id, path: c.path, page: c.page, text: c.text, score, ...(preferRecent ? { mtime: mtimes.get(c.path) || 0 } : {}) });
  });
  hits.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  const out = [];
  const perFileCount = new Map();
  let used = 0;
  for (const h of hits) {
    if (out.length >= k) break;
    const n = perFileCount.get(h.path) || 0;
    if (n >= perFile) continue;
    const text = h.text.length > maxChunkChars ? h.text.slice(0, maxChunkChars) + '…' : h.text;
    if (used + text.length > charBudget && out.length) continue;
    perFileCount.set(h.path, n + 1);
    used += text.length;
    out.push({ ...h, text, score: Math.round(h.score * 100) / 100 });
  }
  return out;
}

// First chunks of a file — used when a taught document has no term overlap
// with the email (e.g. a form or a register the user always wants attached).
export async function leadChunks(path, n = 1, maxChunkChars = 1200) {
  const t = await tx(['files', 'chunks']);
  const f = await reqP(t.objectStore('files').get(path));
  if (!f) return [];
  const ids = (f.chunkIds || []).slice(0, n);
  const recs = await Promise.all(ids.map((id) => reqP(t.objectStore('chunks').get(id))));
  return recs.filter(Boolean).map((c) => ({
    id: c.id, path: c.path, page: c.page, score: 0,
    text: c.text.length > maxChunkChars ? c.text.slice(0, maxChunkChars) + '…' : c.text,
  }));
}

/** Words that mean "the newest version matters" (updates, forecasts, schedules). */
export const RECENCY_WORDS = /\b(updat\w*|forecast\w*|latest|newest|current(ly)?|status|progress|schedule\w*|revis\w*|recent(ly)?|this (week|month)|to date|look-?ahead|projection\w*|estimate at completion|eac|cash ?flow)\b/i;

export async function indexSummary() {
  const stats = (await kvGet('indexStats')) || { n: 0, totalLen: 0 };
  const status = (await kvGet('indexStatus')) || null;
  return { chunks: stats.n, status };
}

/**
 * Empties the index for a rebuild, keeping the documents that can't be read again from a folder: indexed Outlook
 * emails and saved meetings (folderId 'mail' / 'meeting'). Returns how many were kept.
 */
export async function resetIndex(keep = ['mail', 'meeting']) {
  const kept = [];
  for (const r of (await getAll('files')).filter((f) => keep.includes(f.folderId))) {
    const t = await tx(['chunks']);
    const cs = t.objectStore('chunks');
    const chunks = (await Promise.all((r.chunkIds || []).map((id) => reqP(cs.get(id))))).filter(Boolean).sort((a, b) => a.ord - b.ord);
    const { chunkIds, v, ...rec } = r;
    kept.push({ rec, chunks: chunks.map((c) => ({ text: c.text, page: c.page || 0 })) });
  }
  await clear('files', 'chunks', 'terms');
  await kvSet('indexStats', null);
  await kvSet('deadChunks', null);
  const w = new IndexWriter();
  for (const { rec, chunks } of kept) if (chunks.length) await w.addFile(rec, chunks);
  await w.finish();
  return kept.length;
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/indexer.js (220 lines) =====
// Walks the project folders (the mirrors) and keeps the local index current — on this computer, no tokens.
// Incremental: unchanged files (same size and modified time) are skipped.
//
// Files are indexed under their NAS path, e.g. "Projects/2024-017 Alert/Specs/x.pdf", so the OCR
// "deep index" the HP (or a Pi) builds on the NAS lines up with the same files here.

import { getAll, kvGet, kvSet, log, put } from './db.js';
import { IndexWriter, INDEX_VERSION } from './index.js';
import { chunkText, makeMatcher, splitList, extOf } from './text.js';
import { projectFor } from './folders.js';
import { extractFile } from './extract.js';
import { walk, META_DIRS } from './fsutil.js';

const tick = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

// roots: a single directory handle, or [{root, prefix, folderId, projectMode}]. prefix is the folder's
// location on the NAS (without the leading slash).
function normalizeRoots(roots) {
  if (Array.isArray(roots)) return roots;
  return [{ root: roots, prefix: '', folderId: 'local', projectMode: 'subfolders' }];
}

export async function runIndex(roots, settings, { onProgress = () => {}, signal, rootName = 'local' } = {}) {
  const started = Date.now();
  const list = normalizeRoots(roots);
  const exclude = makeMatcher(settings.excludePatterns);
  const include = new Set(splitList(settings.includeExt).map((e) => e.replace(/^\./, '').toLowerCase()));
  const maxBytes = (settings.maxFileMB || 150) * 1024 * 1024;
  const existing = new Map((await getAll('files')).map((f) => [f.path, f]));
  const writer = new IndexWriter();
  const seen = new Set();
  const counts = { scanned: 0, indexed: 0, unchanged: 0, nameOnly: 0, errors: 0, removed: 0, lowText: 0 };
  const errors = [];
  let lastReport = 0;
  const report = (path, force) => {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (!force && now - lastReport < 250) return;
    lastReport = now;
    onProgress({ phase: 'index', path, ...counts });
  };

  for (const { root, prefix, folderId, projectMode } of list) {
    const skip = (path, isDir) => {
      const vp = prefix ? `${prefix}/${path}` : path;
      if (META_DIRS.some((d) => path === d || path.startsWith(d + '/'))) return true;
      return exclude(vp) || (isDir && path.split('/').pop().startsWith('.'));
    };
    for await (const { path: rel, file } of walk(root, { skip, onError: (p, e) => { counts.errors++; errors.push(`${p}: ${e.message}`); } })) {
      if (signal?.aborted) break;
      const path = prefix ? `${prefix}/${rel}` : rel;
      seen.add(path);
      counts.scanned++;
      const prev = existing.get(path);
      const project = projectFor(prefix, rel, projectMode);
      if (prev && prev.v === INDEX_VERSION && prev.size === file.size && prev.mtime === file.lastModified) {
        counts.unchanged++;
        // Same file, new folder settings (e.g. "one project" instead of "one per subfolder"): relabel only.
        if (prev.project !== project || prev.folderId !== folderId) await put('files', { ...prev, project, folderId, rel });
        report(path);
        continue;
      }
      const ext = extOf(file.name);
      const rec = {
        path, rel, folderId, size: file.size, mtime: file.lastModified, ext,
        project,
        indexedAt: Date.now(), method: 'name', chars: 0, note: '',
      };
      let chunks = [];
      if (include.has(ext) && file.size <= maxBytes) {
        try {
          const res = await extractFile(file, { maxChars: settings.maxCharsPerFile });
          chunks = chunkText(res.pages);
          rec.method = res.method;
          rec.chars = res.chars || 0;
          rec.note = res.note || '';
          if (rec.note.startsWith('little text')) counts.lowText++;
          counts.indexed++;
        } catch (err) {
          rec.method = 'error';
          rec.note = String(err.message || err).slice(0, 200);
          counts.errors++;
          errors.push(`${path}: ${rec.note}`);
        }
      } else {
        if (file.size > maxBytes && include.has(ext)) rec.note = 'too large — name only';
        counts.nameOnly++;
      }
      if (!chunks.length) chunks = [{ text: `File: ${path}`, page: 0 }];
      await writer.addFile(rec, chunks);
      report(path, false);
      if (counts.scanned % 20 === 0) await tick();
    }
    if (signal?.aborted) break;
  }

  if (!signal?.aborted) {
    for (const [path, rec] of existing) {
      if (rec.folderId === 'nas' || rec.folderId === 'meeting' || rec.folderId === 'mail') continue; // not from a local folder: kept elsewhere
      if (!seen.has(path)) {
        await writer.removeFile(path);
        counts.removed++;
      }
    }
  }
  await writer.finish();
  const files = await getAll('files');
  const projects = [...new Set(files.map((f) => f.project).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  await kvSet('projects', projects);
  const status = {
    at: Date.now(), root: rootName, ms: Date.now() - started, aborted: !!signal?.aborted,
    files: files.length, folders: list.length, ...counts, errors: errors.slice(0, 50),
  };
  await kvSet('indexStatus', status);
  await log('index', `Indexed ${counts.indexed} changed, ${counts.unchanged} unchanged, ${counts.removed} removed across ${list.length} folder(s)`);
  report('', true);
  return status;
}

// Applies the deep OCR index built on the NAS (by the HP or a Pi). `getFile` returns the gz File fetched
// over WebDAV, or null if there is none yet.
const nasPrefixes = (settings) => (settings.nasFolders || []).map((p) => String(p).replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')).filter(Boolean);

export async function applyDeepIndex(getFile, settings, { onProgress = () => {}, force = false, signal, from = 'NAS' } = {}) {
  let file;
  try {
    file = await getFile();
  } catch (err) {
    return { found: false, error: err.message };
  }
  if (!file) return { applied: 0, found: false };
  if (file.upToDate) return { applied: 0, found: true, upToDate: true, from };
  const last = (await kvGet('deepApplied')) || {};
  if (!force && last.mtime === file.lastModified && last.size === file.size) {
    return { applied: 0, found: true, upToDate: true, from };
  }
  const existing = new Map((await getAll('files')).map((f) => [f.path, f]));
  const writer = new IndexWriter();
  const counts = { records: 0, applied: 0, stale: 0, unmatched: 0, kept: 0, nasOnly: 0, removed: 0 };
  const nasRoots = nasPrefixes(settings);
  const nasRootOf = (p) => nasRoots.find((r) => p === r || p.startsWith(`${r}/`));
  const seenNas = new Set();
  const stream = file.stream()
    .pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('gzip'))
    .pipeThrough(new TextDecoderStream());
  let buf = '';
  const handle = async (line) => {
    if (!line.trim()) return;
    let r;
    try { r = JSON.parse(line); } catch { return; }
    counts.records++;
    let rec = existing.get(r.path);
    // A file that also exists on this computer is indexed from here; the NAS-only path is only for the rest.
    const nasRoot = r.nasOnly && !(rec && rec.folderId !== 'nas') && nasRootOf(r.path);
    if (nasRoot) {
      // A file that is only on the NAS: there is no local copy, so this record is its whole index entry.
      seenNas.add(r.path);
      counts.nasOnly++;
      if (!rec || rec.folderId !== 'nas' || rec.size !== r.size || rec.deepMtime !== r.mtime || force) {
        rec = { path: r.path, rel: r.path.slice(nasRoot.length + 1), folderId: 'nas', size: r.size, mtime: r.mtime, ext: extOf(r.path), project: nasRoot, method: '', chars: 0, note: '' };
      } else {
        counts.kept++;
        return;
      }
    } else {
      if (!rec) { counts.unmatched++; return; }
      if (rec.size !== r.size) { counts.stale++; return; }
    }
    if (!nasRoot && rec.method && rec.method.startsWith('deep:') && rec.deepMtime === r.mtime) { counts.kept++; return; }
    const pages = (r.pages || []).map((p) => [p[0], p[1]]);
    const chars = pages.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (p[1] || '').length, 0);
    if (!chars) { counts.kept++; return; }
    if (!nasRoot && !(chars >= rec.chars * 0.8 || rec.chars < 500)) { counts.kept++; return; }
    // Mailboxes (a .pst unpacked on the HP: one email per "page") may hold much more text than a document.
    const cap = /^(pst|msg)$/.test(r.method || '') ? settings.maxCharsPerMailbox || 8000000 : settings.maxCharsPerFile;
    const capped = [];
    let total = 0;
    for (const [n, t] of pages) {
      if (total >= cap) break;
      capped.push([n, t.slice(0, cap - total)]);
      total += t.length;
    }
    const next = { ...rec, method: `deep:${r.method || 'text'}`, chars, deepMtime: r.mtime, note: '', indexedAt: Date.now() };
    delete next.chunkIds;
    await writer.addFile(next, chunkText(capped));
    counts.applied++;
    if (counts.applied % 10 === 0) onProgress({ phase: 'deep', ...counts });
  };
  const reader = stream.getReader();
  for (;;) {
    if (signal?.aborted) break;
    const { value, done } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    buf += value;
    let nl;
    while ((nl = buf.indexOf('\n')) >= 0) {
      const line = buf.slice(0, nl);
      buf = buf.slice(nl + 1);
      await handle(line);
    }
  }
  if (buf) await handle(buf);
  // NAS-only files that are gone from the NAS (or whose folder was removed from the list) leave the index.
  if (!signal?.aborted) {
    for (const [path, rec] of existing) {
      if (rec.folderId === 'nas' && !seenNas.has(path)) {
        await writer.removeFile(path);
        counts.removed++;
      }
    }
  }
  await writer.finish();
  if (counts.nasOnly || counts.removed) {
    const files = await getAll('files');
    await kvSet('projects', [...new Set(files.map((f) => f.project).filter(Boolean))].sort());
  }
  await kvSet('deepApplied', { mtime: file.lastModified, size: file.size, at: Date.now(), from, ...counts });
  await log('deep', `Deep index from ${from}: ${counts.applied} files improved, ${counts.stale} stale, ${counts.unmatched} not present here`);
  onProgress({ phase: 'deep', done: true, ...counts });
  return { found: true, from, ...counts };
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/llm.js (869 lines) =====
// Provider adapters: Anthropic (Claude, through the official SDK) and Google Gemini (REST), called directly
// from the extension with the user's own API key.
//  - callLLM: one structured call (JSON constrained by a schema) — email drafts, meeting prompts.
//  - runChat: a tool-use loop — the assistant chat, which changes the extension's own settings.
//  - generateText: a plain call with an optional web lookup — meeting standards look-ups.

import { Anthropic } from '../vendor/anthropic-sdk.mjs';
import { getSettings, saveSettings, kvGet, kvSet, kvAdd } from './db.js';
import { nasCreds } from './nas.js';
import { basicAuthHeader } from './util.js';

export class LLMError extends Error {
  constructor(kind, message, status) {
    super(message);
    // auth | rate | declined | truncated | network | bad-request | server | parse | config | stopped | paused | budget
    this.kind = kind;
    this.status = status;
  }
}

// ---------------- kill switch and spending limit ----------------
// Every paid call (Claude or Gemini) goes through aiGate() and track(): "Stop AI" aborts the calls in flight in
// every open part of the extension and refuses new ones until "Resume AI"; a daily limit does the same once
// today's estimated spend reaches it.

const inFlight = new Set();

/** An abort signal for one call, cancelled by its own signal or by Stop AI. */
function track(signal) {
  const c = new AbortController();
  inFlight.add(c);
  const onAbort = () => c.abort();
  if (signal) {
    if (signal.aborted) c.abort();
    else signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
  }
  return {
    signal: c.signal,
    done: () => {
      inFlight.delete(c);
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
    },
  };
}

/** Cancels every AI call in progress in this page. */
export function abortAllAI() {
  for (const c of inFlight) c.abort();
  inFlight.clear();
}

try {
  chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((m) => {
    if (m?.type === 'ai.stopAll') abortAllAI();
    return false;
  });
} catch {
  /* not in an extension page */
}

/** Stop AI everywhere: saves the switch, cancels the calls here and tells the other pages to cancel theirs. */
export async function stopAllAI() {
  await saveSettings({ aiPaused: true });
  abortAllAI();
  try {
    await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ to: 'all', type: 'ai.stopAll' });
  } catch {
    /* no other page open */
  }
}

export async function resumeAI() {
  await saveSettings({ aiPaused: false });
  try {
    await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ to: 'all', type: 'ai.resumed' });
  } catch {
    /* no other page open */
  }
}

function today() {
  const d = new Date();
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export async function spentToday() {
  return (await kvGet(`spend:${today()}`)) || 0;
}

// Spending is recorded per day and, for the month, per feature (settings._tag: 'email', 'check', 'meeting',
// 'research', 'minutes', 'transcription', 'chat', 'agent', 'lookup', …), so Settings can show where it goes.
const round5 = (n) => Math.round(n * 1e5) / 1e5;
async function recordSpend(usage, model, settings) {
  const c = costOf(usage, model, settings);
  if (!c) return;
  try {
    await kvAdd(`spend:${today()}`, c);
    const month = today().slice(0, 7);
    await kvAdd(`spendtag:${month}:${settings?._tag || 'other'}`, c);
    const tags = new Set((await kvGet(`spendtags:${month}`)) || []);
    if (!tags.has(settings?._tag || 'other')) await kvSet(`spendtags:${month}`, [...tags, settings?._tag || 'other']);
  } catch {
    /* the estimate is best effort */
  }
}

/** {today, month, byFeature: {email: $, …}} — estimated from token counts. */
export async function spendReport() {
  const month = today().slice(0, 7);
  const tags = (await kvGet(`spendtags:${month}`)) || [];
  const byFeature = {};
  for (const t of tags) byFeature[t] = (await kvGet(`spendtag:${month}:${t}`)) || 0;
  return { today: await spentToday(), month: round5(Object.values(byFeature).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)), byFeature };
}

/** Throws if AI is stopped or today's limit is reached. Reads the switch fresh, so it applies at once. */
export async function aiGate() {
  const s = await getSettings();
  if (s.aiPaused) throw new LLMError('paused', 'AI is stopped (kill switch). Click "Resume AI" to use it again.');
  const limit = Number(s.dailyBudgetUsd) || 0;
  if (limit > 0) {
    const spent = await spentToday();
    if (spent >= limit) {
      throw new LLMError('budget', `Today's AI spending limit ($${limit.toFixed(2)}) is reached (about $${spent.toFixed(2)} spent). Raise it in Settings → Accuracy & safety, or wait until tomorrow.`);
    }
  }
  return s;
}

// USD per 1M tokens: [input, output, cacheRead]. Longest matching prefix wins.
const PRICES = {
  'claude-fable-5-1': [10, 50, 1],
  'claude-opus-5-5': [4, 20, 0.2],
  'claude-opus-5': [5, 25, 0.5],
  'claude-opus-4': [5, 25, 0.5],
  'claude-sonnet-5-5': [2, 10, 0.2],
  'claude-sonnet-5': [2, 10, 0.2],
  'claude-sonnet-4': [3, 15, 0.3],
  'claude-haiku-5-5': [0.1, 0.5, 0.01],
  'claude-haiku-4': [1, 5, 0.1],
  'gemini-2.5-pro': [1.25, 10, 0.31],
  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': [0.1, 0.4, 0.025],
  'gemini-2.5-flash': [0.3, 2.5, 0.075],
  'gemini-3-pro': [2, 12, 0.2],
  'gemini-3-flash': [0.5, 3, 0.05],
};

export function priceFor(model, settings = {}) {
  if (settings.priceInPerM != null && settings.priceOutPerM != null && settings.priceInPerM !== '' && settings.priceOutPerM !== '') {
    const i = Number(settings.priceInPerM);
    return [i, Number(settings.priceOutPerM), i / 10];
  }
  const m = String(model || '').replace(/^models\//, '');
  const key = Object.keys(PRICES)
    .filter((k) => m.startsWith(k))
    .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)[0];
  if (key) return PRICES[key];
  // A model not in the table (a newer Gemini, one typed by hand) still counts: its family's price, or the dearest
  // of its provider, so the spending panel and the daily limit never see it as free.
  if (/^gemini/i.test(m)) return /pro/i.test(m) ? PRICES['gemini-3-pro'] : /lite/i.test(m) ? PRICES['gemini-2.5-flash-lite'] : /flash/i.test(m) ? PRICES['gemini-3-flash'] : PRICES['gemini-3-pro'];
  if (/^claude/i.test(m)) return /haiku/i.test(m) ? PRICES['claude-haiku-4'] : /sonnet/i.test(m) ? PRICES['claude-sonnet-4'] : PRICES['claude-fable-5-1'];
  return m ? PRICES['claude-fable-5-1'] : null;
}

export function costOf(usage, model, settings) {
  if (String(model || '').startsWith('local:')) return 0; // your own model on the HP: free
  const p = priceFor(model, settings);
  if (!p) return null;
  const [pin, pout, pread] = p;
  const cost = ((usage.input || 0) * pin + (usage.cacheWrite || 0) * pin * 1.25 + (usage.cacheRead || 0) * pread + (usage.output || 0) * pout) / 1e6;
  return Math.round(cost * 1e5) / 1e5;
}

export function activeModel(settings, provider = settings.provider) {
  if (provider === 'local') return settings.localModel;
  return provider === 'gemini' ? settings.geminiModel : settings.anthropicModel;
}

export function hasKey(settings, provider = settings.provider) {
  if (provider === 'local') return !!(settings.localUrl && settings.localModel);
  return provider === 'gemini' ? !!settings.geminiKey : !!settings.anthropicKey;
}

/**
 * The local model (on the HP) for a job the user gave it in Settings ('priorities', 'invoices', 'check'), else
 * the given cloud provider.
 */
export function providerForJob(settings, job, fallback = settings.provider) {
  return settings.localFor?.[job] && hasKey(settings, 'local') ? 'local' : pickProvider(settings, fallback);
}

// ---------------- the local model (OpenAI-compatible: Ollama, LM Studio, llama.cpp) ----------------

function localBase(settings) {
  const u = String(settings.localUrl || '').trim();
  return u.endsWith('/') ? u : `${u}/`;
}

async function localHeaders(settings) {
  const h = { 'content-type': 'application/json' };
  if (settings.localAuth === 'nas') {
    // The NAS login goes only to the NAS address itself (its /.llm/ proxy), never to another server.
    const c = await nasCreds().catch(() => null);
    let sameHost = false;
    try {
      const { CONFIG } = await import('./config-store.js');
      sameHost = new URL(localBase(settings)).origin === new URL(CONFIG.serverUrl).origin && /^https:/.test(CONFIG.serverUrl);
    } catch {
      sameHost = false;
    }
    if (sameHost && !c) throw new LLMError('config', 'The local model needs the NAS login: open "NAS files" once and enter the password (or tick "remember" there).');
    if (c && sameHost) h.authorization = basicAuthHeader(c.username, c.password);
  } else if (settings.localAuth === 'key' && settings.localKey) h.authorization = `Bearer ${settings.localKey}`;
  return h;
}

async function callLocal(settings, { system, parts, schema, maxTokens, signal }) {
  const url = new URL('v1/chat/completions', localBase(settings)).href;
  const headers = await localHeaders(settings);
  const body = {
    model: settings.localModel,
    messages: [
      { role: 'system', content: `${system}${schema ? `\n\nReply with one JSON object only (no other text), matching this JSON schema:\n${JSON.stringify(schema)}` : ''}` },
      { role: 'user', content: parts.map((p) => (typeof p === 'string' ? p : p.text || '')).join('\n\n') },
    ],
    temperature: 0,
    max_tokens: maxTokens || 4000,
    stream: false,
    ...(schema ? { response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: { name: 'result', schema, strict: true } } } : {}),
  };
  await aiGate();
  const call = track(signal);
  try {
    let data;
    try {
      data = await httpJson(url, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: call.signal }, { retries: 1 });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.kind === 'auth') throw new LLMError('auth', `The local model refused the login (${err.status}): check Settings → AI → Local model (NAS login or API key).`, err.status);
      // Older servers don't know json_schema: ask for JSON in the prompt only.
      if (!schema || err.kind !== 'bad-request') throw err;
      delete body.response_format;
      data = await httpJson(url, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: call.signal }, { retries: 1 });
    }
    const text = String(data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '').replace(/^\s*<think>[\s\S]*?<\/think>\s*/, '');
    const usage = { input: data?.usage?.prompt_tokens || 0, output: data?.usage?.completion_tokens || 0, cacheRead: 0, cacheWrite: 0 };
    return { data: schema ? parseJson(text) : text, text, usage, model: `local:${data?.model || settings.localModel}`, provider: 'local' };
  } finally {
    call.done();
  }
}

/** The models the local server offers (doubles as a connection test). */
export async function listLocalModels(settings) {
  if (!settings.localUrl) throw new LLMError('config', 'Enter the local model address first');
  const data = await httpJson(new URL('v1/models', localBase(settings)).href, { headers: await localHeaders(settings) }, { retries: 0 });
  return (data?.data || data?.models || []).map((m) => m.id || m.name || m.model).filter(Boolean);
}

/** The provider for a job, falling back to whichever key exists. */
export function pickProvider(settings, preferred) {
  const want = preferred || settings.provider;
  if (hasKey(settings, want)) return want;
  const other = want === 'gemini' ? 'anthropic' : 'gemini';
  return hasKey(settings, other) ? other : want;
}

export function parseJson(text) {
  let t = String(text || '').trim();
  t = t.replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, '').replace(/```\s*$/, '');
  try {
    return JSON.parse(t);
  } catch {
    const a = t.indexOf('{');
    const b = t.lastIndexOf('}');
    if (a >= 0 && b > a) {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(t.slice(a, b + 1));
      } catch {
        /* fall through */
      }
    }
    throw new LLMError('parse', 'Model returned text that is not valid JSON');
  }
}

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

async function httpJson(url, init, { retries = 2 } = {}) {
  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
    let res;
    try {
      res = await fetch(url, init);
    } catch (err) {
      if (init?.signal?.aborted) throw new LLMError('stopped', 'Stopped.');
      if (attempt < retries) {
        await sleep(1500 * (attempt + 1));
        continue;
      }
      throw new LLMError('network', `Network error: ${err.message}`);
    }
    let body = null;
    const raw = await res.text();
    try {
      body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
    } catch {
      body = { raw };
    }
    if (res.ok) return body;
    const msg = body?.error?.message || body?.message || raw.slice(0, 300) || res.statusText;
    if ((res.status === 429 || res.status >= 500) && attempt < retries) {
      const ra = Number(res.headers.get('retry-after'));
      await sleep(Number.isFinite(ra) && ra > 0 ? Math.min(ra, 30) * 1000 : 2000 * (attempt + 1));
      continue;
    }
    if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) throw new LLMError('auth', `API key rejected (${res.status}): ${msg}`, res.status);
    if (res.status === 429 && /free.?tier/i.test(msg)) {
      throw new LLMError('rate', `This Gemini key is on Google's free tier and its free quota for this model is used up. To use the paid tier, turn on billing for the key's project in Google AI Studio (aistudio.google.com → Get API key → Set up billing), or pick another model. (${msg.slice(0, 160)})`, res.status);
    }
    if (res.status === 429) throw new LLMError('rate', `Rate limited: ${msg}`, res.status);
    if (res.status >= 500) throw new LLMError('server', `Provider error ${res.status}: ${msg}`, res.status);
    if (/api key not valid|api_key_invalid/i.test(msg)) throw new LLMError('auth', msg, res.status);
    throw new LLMError('bad-request', `Request rejected (${res.status}): ${msg}`, res.status);
  }
}

// ---------------- Anthropic (official SDK) ----------------

const FALLBACK_MODELS = /^claude-(opus-5-5|opus-5|fable-5-1|sonnet-5-5)$/;
const FALLBACK_BETA = 'server-side-fallback-2026-07-01';

function anthropicClient(settings) {
  return new Anthropic({
    apiKey: settings.anthropicKey,
    baseURL: (settings.anthropicBaseUrl || 'https://api.anthropic.com').replace(/\/+$/, ''),
    dangerouslyAllowBrowser: true, // the key is the user's own, kept in this browser profile
    maxRetries: 2,
    timeout: 600000, // explicit, so the SDK does not insist on streaming for large max_tokens
  });
}

function anthropicError(err) {
  if (err instanceof LLMError) return err;
  if (err instanceof Anthropic.APIUserAbortError || err?.name === 'AbortError') return new LLMError('stopped', 'Stopped.');
  const status = err?.status;
  const msg = err?.error?.error?.message || err?.message || String(err);
  if (err instanceof Anthropic.AuthenticationError || err instanceof Anthropic.PermissionDeniedError) {
    return new LLMError('auth', `API key rejected (${status}): ${msg}`, status);
  }
  if (err instanceof Anthropic.RateLimitError) return new LLMError('rate', `Rate limited: ${msg}`, status);
  if (err instanceof Anthropic.InternalServerError) return new LLMError('server', `Provider error ${status}: ${msg}`, status);
  if (err instanceof Anthropic.APIConnectionError) return new LLMError('network', `Network error: ${msg}`);
  if (err instanceof Anthropic.APIError) return new LLMError('bad-request', `Request rejected (${status}): ${msg}`, status);
  return new LLMError('network', msg);
}

function anthropicUsage(u) {
  return {
    input: u?.input_tokens || 0,
    output: u?.output_tokens || 0,
    cacheRead: u?.cache_read_input_tokens || 0,
    cacheWrite: u?.cache_creation_input_tokens || 0,
  };
}

function addUsage(total, u) {
  for (const k of ['input', 'output', 'cacheRead', 'cacheWrite']) total[k] = (total[k] || 0) + (u[k] || 0);
  return total;
}

function declined(data, usage) {
  const e = new LLMError('declined', `The model declined this request${data.stop_details?.category ? ` (${data.stop_details.category})` : ''}.`);
  e.usage = usage;
  return e;
}

/**
 * One Messages API request with graceful degradation: if the account or model rejects an optional
 * feature (server-side fallback, effort, structured output), retry without it.
 * opts: {fallbacks, effort, schema} — mutated as features are dropped.
 */
async function anthropicCreate(client, settings, params, opts, outerSignal) {
  await aiGate();
  const call = track(outerSignal);
  try {
    const data = await anthropicCreateOnce(client, settings, params, opts, call.signal);
    recordSpend(anthropicUsage(data.usage), data.model || params.model, settings);
    return data;
  } finally {
    call.done();
  }
}

async function anthropicCreateOnce(client, settings, params, opts, signal) {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4; attempt++) {
    const body = { ...params };
    const outputConfig = { ...(params.output_config || {}) };
    if (opts.effort) outputConfig.effort = opts.effort;
    if (opts.schema) outputConfig.format = { type: 'json_schema', schema: opts.schema };
    if (Object.keys(outputConfig).length) body.output_config = outputConfig;
    else delete body.output_config;
    try {
      if (opts.fallbacks) {
        return await client.beta.messages.create({ ...body, fallbacks: 'default', betas: [FALLBACK_BETA] }, { signal });
      }
      return await client.messages.create(body, { signal });
    } catch (e) {
      const err = anthropicError(e);
      if (err.kind === 'bad-request') {
        const m = err.message.toLowerCase();
        if (opts.fallbacks && /fallback|beta/.test(m)) {
          opts.fallbacks = false;
          continue;
        }
        if (opts.effort && /effort/.test(m)) {
          opts.effort = null;
          continue;
        }
        if (opts.schema && /output_config|format|schema/.test(m)) {
          opts.schema = null;
          continue;
        }
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }
  throw new LLMError('bad-request', 'Anthropic request failed after retries');
}

function anthropicOpts(settings, model, effort, schema) {
  return {
    fallbacks: settings.anthropicFallbacks !== false && FALLBACK_MODELS.test(model),
    effort: effort || settings.effort || null,
    schema: schema || null,
  };
}

async function callAnthropic(settings, { system, parts, schema, effort, maxTokens, cacheParts = [], model: modelOverride, signal }) {
  const model = modelOverride || settings.anthropicModel;
  const client = anthropicClient(settings);
  const opts = anthropicOpts(settings, model, effort, schema);
  let max = maxTokens || 8000;
  const usage = {};
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
    const data = await anthropicCreate(
      client,
      settings,
      {
        model,
        max_tokens: max,
        system: [{ type: 'text', text: system, cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral' } }],
        messages: [
          {
            role: 'user',
            content: parts.map((text, i) => (cacheParts.includes(i) ? { type: 'text', text, cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral' } } : { type: 'text', text })),
          },
        ],
      },
      opts,
      signal,
    );
    addUsage(usage, anthropicUsage(data.usage));
    if (data.stop_reason === 'refusal') throw declined(data, usage);
    if (data.stop_reason === 'max_tokens') {
      if (max < 32000) {
        max *= 2;
        continue;
      }
      throw new LLMError('truncated', 'Response hit the output limit');
    }
    const text = (data.content || []).filter((b) => b.type === 'text').map((b) => b.text).join('');
    return { data: parseJson(text), usage, model: data.model || model, provider: 'anthropic' };
  }
  throw new LLMError('truncated', 'Response hit the output limit');
}

// ---------------- Gemini (REST) ----------------

function geminiBase(settings) {
  return (settings.geminiBaseUrl || 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com').replace(/\/+$/, '');
}

function toGeminiSchema(s) {
  if (!s || typeof s !== 'object') return s;
  const out = {};
  if (Array.isArray(s.type)) {
    // Gemini takes one type per field (+ nullable), not a list.
    out.type = String(s.type.find((t) => t !== 'null') || 'string').toUpperCase();
    if (s.type.includes('null')) out.nullable = true;
  } else if (s.type) out.type = String(s.type).toUpperCase();
  if (s.description) out.description = s.description;
  if (s.enum) out.enum = s.enum;
  if (s.properties) {
    out.properties = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(s.properties)) out.properties[k] = toGeminiSchema(v);
    out.propertyOrdering = Object.keys(s.properties);
  }
  if (s.required) out.required = s.required;
  if (s.items) out.items = toGeminiSchema(s.items);
  return out;
}

function geminiThinking(model, effort) {
  if (/gemini-2\.5/.test(model)) return { thinkingBudget: effort === 'high' ? -1 : effort === 'medium' ? 2048 : 256 };
  if (/gemini-[3-9]/.test(model)) return { thinkingLevel: effort === 'low' ? 'low' : 'high' };
  return null;
}

let geminiModelCache = null; // {key, model}

/** 'auto' → the newest general-purpose Gemini Flash model this key can use (looked up once). */
export async function resolveGeminiModel(settings) {
  const m = String(settings.geminiModel || '').replace(/^models\//, '');
  if (m && m !== 'auto') return m;
  if (geminiModelCache?.key === settings.geminiKey) return geminiModelCache.model;
  let models = [];
  try {
    models = await listModels(settings, 'gemini');
  } catch (err) {
    // A wrong key must say so, not carry on silently with a guessed model.
    if (err.kind === 'auth' || err.kind === 'config') throw err;
    models = [];
  }
  const model = pickGeminiFlash(models) || 'gemini-2.5-flash';
  geminiModelCache = { key: settings.geminiKey, model };
  return model;
}

/**
 * Newest stable "gemini-X.Y-flash" (previews only if there is no stable one — they get withdrawn without
 * notice); never the lite/image/tts/live/audio variants.
 */
export function pickGeminiFlash(models) {
  const scored = [];
  for (const name of models) {
    if (/tts|image|audio|live|embedding/i.test(name)) continue; // not text models
    const m = /^gemini-(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)-flash(-preview(?:-[\w-]+)?|-\d{3}|-latest)?$/.exec(name);
    if (!m) continue;
    const stable = !m[2] || /^-\d{3}$/.test(m[2]);
    scored.push([stable ? 1 : 0, Number(m[1]), name]);
  }
  scored.sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0] || b[1] - a[1]);
  return scored[0]?.[2] || null;
}

function geminiUsage(um = {}) {
  return {
    input: Math.max(0, (um.promptTokenCount || 0) - (um.cachedContentTokenCount || 0)),
    output: (um.candidatesTokenCount || 0) + (um.thoughtsTokenCount || 0),
    cacheRead: um.cachedContentTokenCount || 0,
    cacheWrite: 0,
  };
}

async function geminiGenerate(settings, model, body, outerSignal) {
  await aiGate();
  const call = track(outerSignal);
  try {
    const data = await httpJson(`${geminiBase(settings)}/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(model)}:generateContent`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-goog-api-key': settings.geminiKey },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal: call.signal,
    });
    recordSpend(geminiUsage(data.usageMetadata), data.modelVersion || model, settings);
    return data;
  } finally {
    call.done();
  }
}

/** Gemini audio transcription (lib/stt.js) uses the same kill switch, limit and spend record. */
export function geminiRequest(settings, model, body, signal) {
  return geminiGenerate(settings, model, body, signal);
}

function geminiCandidate(data, usage) {
  if (data.promptFeedback?.blockReason) {
    const e = new LLMError('declined', `Gemini blocked the request (${data.promptFeedback.blockReason}).`);
    e.usage = usage;
    throw e;
  }
  const cand = data.candidates?.[0];
  if (!cand) throw new LLMError('server', 'Gemini returned no candidates');
  if (['SAFETY', 'RECITATION', 'PROHIBITED_CONTENT', 'BLOCKLIST', 'SPII'].includes(cand.finishReason)) {
    const e = new LLMError('declined', `Gemini stopped the response (${cand.finishReason}).`);
    e.usage = usage;
    throw e;
  }
  return cand;
}

const geminiText = (cand) => (cand.content?.parts || []).filter((p) => !p.thought && typeof p.text === 'string').map((p) => p.text).join('');

async function callGemini(settings, { system, parts, schema, effort, maxTokens, signal }) {
  const model = await resolveGeminiModel(settings);
  const opts = { thinking: geminiThinking(model, effort || settings.effort), schema: true };
  let max = maxTokens || 8192;
  const usage = {};
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++) {
    const generationConfig = { responseMimeType: 'application/json', maxOutputTokens: max };
    if (opts.schema) generationConfig.responseSchema = toGeminiSchema(schema);
    if (opts.thinking) generationConfig.thinkingConfig = opts.thinking;
    const body = {
      systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: system }] },
      contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: parts.map((text) => ({ text })) }],
      generationConfig,
    };
    let data;
    try {
      data = await geminiGenerate(settings, model, body, signal);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.kind === 'bad-request') {
        const m = err.message.toLowerCase();
        if (opts.thinking && /thinking/.test(m)) {
          opts.thinking = null;
          continue;
        }
        if (opts.schema && /schema/.test(m)) {
          opts.schema = false;
          continue;
        }
      }
      throw err;
    }
    addUsage(usage, geminiUsage(data.usageMetadata));
    const cand = geminiCandidate(data, usage);
    if (cand.finishReason === 'MAX_TOKENS') {
      if (max < 32768) {
        max *= 2;
        continue;
      }
      throw new LLMError('truncated', 'Response hit the output limit');
    }
    return { data: parseJson(geminiText(cand)), usage, model: data.modelVersion || model, provider: 'gemini' };
  }
  throw new LLMError('bad-request', 'Gemini request failed after retries');
}

/** request: {system, parts: [string], schema, effort?, maxTokens?, cacheParts?, provider?, model?, signal?} */
export async function callLLM(settings, request) {
  const provider = request.provider || settings.provider;
  if (provider === 'local') {
    if (!hasKey(settings, 'local')) throw new LLMError('config', 'The local model is not set up (Settings → AI → Local model)');
    return callLocal(settings, request);
  }
  if (!hasKey(settings, provider)) throw new LLMError('config', `No ${provider === 'gemini' ? 'Gemini' : 'Anthropic'} API key set`);
  return provider === 'gemini' ? callGemini(settings, request) : callAnthropic(settings, request);
}

// ---------------- plain text with optional web lookup ----------------

/**
 * One call that may search the web (Claude's web_search tool / Gemini's Google Search grounding).
 * Returns {text, sources: [{url, title}], usage, model, provider}.
 */
export async function generateText(settings, { provider: p, system, prompt, web = false, effort, maxTokens = 2000, signal }) {
  const provider = p || settings.provider;
  if (provider === 'local') throw new LLMError('config', 'The local model cannot search the web');
  if (!hasKey(settings, provider)) throw new LLMError('config', `No ${provider === 'gemini' ? 'Gemini' : 'Anthropic'} API key set`);
  if (provider === 'gemini') {
    const model = await resolveGeminiModel(settings);
    const body = {
      systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: system }] },
      contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: prompt }] }],
      generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: maxTokens, ...(geminiThinking(model, effort || 'low') ? { thinkingConfig: geminiThinking(model, effort || 'low') } : {}) },
      ...(web ? { tools: [{ google_search: {} }] } : {}),
    };
    const data = await geminiGenerate(settings, model, body, signal);
    const usage = geminiUsage(data.usageMetadata);
    const cand = geminiCandidate(data, usage);
    const chunks = cand.groundingMetadata?.groundingChunks || [];
    const sources = chunks.map((c) => c.web).filter(Boolean).map((w) => ({ url: w.uri, title: w.title || w.uri }));
    return { text: geminiText(cand), sources, usage, model: data.modelVersion || model, provider };
  }
  const model = settings.anthropicModel;
  const client = anthropicClient(settings);
  const opts = anthropicOpts(settings, model, effort || 'low', null);
  const messages = [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }];
  const usage = {};
  const sources = [];
  let text = '';
  for (let turn = 0; turn < 4; turn++) {
    const data = await anthropicCreate(
      client,
      settings,
      {
        model,
        max_tokens: maxTokens,
        system,
        messages,
        ...(web ? { tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20260209', name: 'web_search', max_uses: 3 }] } : {}),
      },
      opts,
      signal,
    );
    addUsage(usage, anthropicUsage(data.usage));
    if (data.stop_reason === 'refusal') throw declined(data, usage);
    for (const b of data.content || []) {
      if (b.type !== 'text') continue;
      text += b.text;
      for (const c of b.citations || []) if (c.url && !sources.some((s) => s.url === c.url)) sources.push({ url: c.url, title: c.title || c.url, quote: c.cited_text || '' });
    }
    if (data.stop_reason !== 'pause_turn') return { text, sources, usage, model: data.model || model, provider };
    messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: data.content }); // the server resumes its search loop
  }
  return { text, sources, usage, model, provider };
}

// ---------------- tool-use loop (assistant chat) ----------------

/**
 * Runs a conversation with client-side tools until the model answers.
 * tools: [{name, description, input_schema, run: async (input) => result}]
 * history: [{role: 'user'|'assistant', text}] — earlier turns as plain text (keeps every call small).
 * onStep({type:'tool', name, input, result}) is called after each tool runs.
 * Returns {text, usage, model, provider, steps}.
 */
export async function runChat(settings, { provider: p, system, history, tools, effort, maxTokens = 4000, maxTurns = 12, onStep = () => {}, onText = () => {}, trim = null, signal }) {
  const provider = p || settings.provider;
  if (provider === 'local') throw new LLMError('config', 'The chat and the agent need Claude or Gemini');
  if (!hasKey(settings, provider)) throw new LLMError('config', `No ${provider === 'gemini' ? 'Gemini' : 'Anthropic'} API key set`);
  const byName = new Map(tools.map((t) => [t.name, t]));
  const steps = [];
  const usage = {};
  const checkStop = () => {
    if (signal?.aborted) throw new LLMError('stopped', 'Stopped.');
  };
  // A tool may return {image: {mime, data}} (a screenshot): sent to the model as a picture, not as text.
  const split = (result) => {
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object' || !result.image) return { data: result, image: null };
    const { image, ...rest } = result;
    return { data: rest, image };
  };
  const runTool = async (name, input) => {
    checkStop();
    const t = byName.get(name);
    let result;
    try {
      result = t ? await t.run(input || {}) : { error: `unknown tool ${name}` };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof LLMError && (err.kind === 'stopped' || err.kind === 'paused')) throw err;
      result = { error: err.message || String(err) };
    }
    const { data, image } = split(result);
    const step = { type: 'tool', name, input, result: data, image: !!image };
    steps.push(step);
    onStep(step);
    return result;
  };
  // trim = {tools: ['read_page', 'screenshot'], keep: 2}: older results of those tools are replaced by a short
  // note before each call, so a long task doesn't resend every old page (the newest ones are kept).
  const trimmed = [];
  const noteTrim = (name, ref) => {
    if (!trim || !trim.tools.includes(name)) return;
    trimmed.push(ref);
    while (trimmed.length > (trim.keep ?? 2)) trimmed.shift().stub();
  };

  if (provider === 'gemini') {
    const model = await resolveGeminiModel(settings);
    const contents = history.map((m) => ({ role: m.role === 'assistant' ? 'model' : 'user', parts: [{ text: m.text }] }));
    const thinking = geminiThinking(model, effort || 'medium');
    for (let turn = 0; turn < maxTurns; turn++) {
      checkStop();
      const data = await geminiGenerate(
        settings,
        model,
        {
          systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: system }] },
          contents,
          tools: [
            {
              functionDeclarations: tools.map((t) => ({
                name: t.name,
                description: t.description,
                // Gemini rejects an OBJECT with no properties, so a tool without inputs gets no schema.
                ...(Object.keys(t.input_schema?.properties || {}).length ? { parameters: toGeminiSchema(t.input_schema) } : {}),
              })),
            },
          ],
          generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: maxTokens, ...(thinking ? { thinkingConfig: thinking } : {}) },
        },
        signal,
      );
      addUsage(usage, geminiUsage(data.usageMetadata));
      const cand = geminiCandidate(data, usage);
      const calls = (cand.content?.parts || []).filter((x) => x.functionCall);
      if (!calls.length) return { text: geminiText(cand), usage, model: data.modelVersion || model, provider, steps };
      const said = geminiText(cand);
      if (said.trim()) onText(said);
      contents.push(cand.content); // echoed back unchanged (it carries Gemini's thought signatures)
      const responses = [];
      const pictures = [];
      for (const c of calls) {
        const { data: result, image } = split(await runTool(c.functionCall.name, c.functionCall.args));
        const part = { functionResponse: { name: c.functionCall.name, response: { result } } };
        responses.push(part);
        if (image) {
          const pic = { inline_data: { mime_type: image.mime, data: image.data } };
          pictures.push(pic);
          noteTrim(c.functionCall.name, { stub: () => { delete pic.inline_data; pic.text = '[an earlier screenshot, removed to save tokens]'; } });
        } else {
          noteTrim(c.functionCall.name, { stub: () => { part.functionResponse.response = { result: '[an earlier result, removed to save tokens — call the tool again for the current state]' }; } });
        }
      }
      contents.push({ role: 'user', parts: [...responses, ...pictures] });
    }
    return { text: 'I stopped after too many steps. Tell me what to do next.', usage, model, provider, steps };
  }
